1. `_prepare` (as the scan's steps 2 and 3, logged as "Ending the session"). Without full access → nothing read; go to 4 and answer `ScanNoAccess` (it ends at once). A `PlatformException` there is logged; while it is kept (step 3's count) it is rethrown and nothing changes.
2. `_stillSession` (another session meanwhile → `ScanShootingOff`, nothing written), then `_scanVolumes` with `caps: generations`: only the volumes in the map, each as the scan does (a rebuilt volume is resynced, an unmounted one skipped), with `captured` lowered to its cap, so a video added after the tap is never kept and the baseline moves no higher. A recording still pending at the tap is not added (it holds the baseline). An empty map scans nothing.
3. **A volume failed** (or step 1 threw): `_keepAfterFailure` notes it for this session, in this process. It counts only when it comes at least `endRetryGap` (5 minutes, by the clock) from the last counted failure (a clock set back by 5 minutes or more counts at once, so it does not hold the count off until the clock catches up; a smaller step back is still "too soon"); a closer one is logged as `Ending the session failed again, too soon: not counted` (one start tries the end through the copy's write, the lifecycle's scan and To tag's at once). Fewer than `maxEndAttempts` (3) counted → the session **and the action are kept** for the next trigger, logged as `Ending the session: <n> volumes failed; kept for the next try (<k> of 3)`; meanwhile `BackgroundSync` writes the copy off. The third counted failure (at least 10 minutes after the first) → it goes on to 4 with what could be read, logged as `Ending the session after 3 attempts: <n> volumes not read`; when step 1 threw, nothing was read, and every volume of the tap and of the session is reported as failed (so the lifecycle does not clear the notification).
4. `PhoneStateRepository.endSession(since)`: in one transaction, delete `shootingMode` only if it is still that session (a session switched off and on again meanwhile stays). The count and its time reset after it returns, not before (a database failure there throws, keeps the count, and the next try is not the first again). A step 1 failure that ends the session on its third try is counted once, not twice. Logged as `Ended the session from the shade: off` (or `left on`). The caller clears the action.
5. Returns the `ScanDone` of step 2 (or `ScanNoAccess`).

**Stale-copy windows** (both heal at the next change or resume): a write that joined the loop just before a later tap does not see it until the next pass; an "on" write already on its way when the receiver writes "off" can land after it.

### Checking clips still exist (`lib/data/media/media_verifier.dart`, D-72)

Nothing calls it yet (Tasks 9 and 10 will, where clips are shown; Phase 10 calls `verifyAll` after an import).

1. `MediaVerifier.verify(clips)` keeps only the clip ids and queues the check on `MediaWorkQueue`; `verifyAll()` queues it with `ClipsRepository.allClipIds()`. No ids: an empty `VerifyOutcome`, the bridge is not called.
2. `bridge.accessReport()`.
3. `ClipsRepository.clipsByIds(ids)` reads the clips as they are now (a scan before it in the queue may have remapped them). Ids that no longer exist are `skipped`. Picked clips are checked by address whatever the access (see "Picked clips: pictures, checks and the grant clean-up"). Not `full` → every other clip `skipped`, the library is not asked.
4. `bridge.volumes()` and `PhoneStateRepository.volumeVersions()` (only when there is a clip left to check this way). A picked clip with no address, a detached one (`mediaStoreId` null or ≤ 0) and a clip whose volume is not listed as mounted are `skipped`.
5. Per volume: `LibraryResync.catchUp(volume, recorded versions)` (the scanner's rule). After a rebuild it matches the volume's clips by fingerprint and writes the result (`applyRebuildMatch`); the group's clips the match lost are `missing` (detached, and out of To tag and Skipped unless in a reel), the rest are read again (`clipsByIds`). Then their numbers go to `bridge.videosByIds(volume, batch)` in batches of 500. Per clip: a row that is `sameVideo` → found; a row that is not → "other video"; no row → gone. If anything about the volume throws (a `PlatformException`, or any other error), its clips not already lost are `skipped` (logged by volume name and code or type).
6. One write, `ClipsRepository.applyVerification(found:, gone:, otherVideo:, flagOnly:)`, in one transaction: detach the other-video clips; flag gone and `flagOnly` (`missing`) and found (not missing), with `lastVerifiedAt`; take gone and other-video clips out of To tag and Skipped, then the orphan sweep (a clip in a reel stays, flagged). `flagOnly` is the unreadable picked clips (D-116): flagged, never taken out of any list.
7. Returns `VerifyOutcome(found, missing: gone ∪ other video ∪ lost in a rebuild, skipped)`.

A failing `accessReport` or `volumes()` throws before anything is written.

**Zone sync** (`lib/data/media/zone_sync.dart › ZoneSync.sync`, `zoneSyncProvider`; `MediaLifecycle` calls it before the scan, see "App start and resume", and the scan and `addEarlier` call it again inside their own queued job, right before they read the timeline; a second sync changes nothing that is stored): `bridge.pendingZoneChanges()` and `bridge.currentZoneId()`, then `ZoneRepository.recordAll(native entries + (now, current zone))`, then, only if that succeeded and the native list was not empty, `bridge.clearZoneChangesUpTo(the newest native atMs)`. Any failure throws and leaves the native list as it was.

### Adding earlier videos (`MediaScanner.countEarlier` / `countEarlierIn` / `addEarlier`, D-73, D-78)

`EarlierVideosController` (`features/tagging/view_models/earlier_videos_controller.dart`, D-75) calls it; the sheet is under "Add earlier videos" in the To tag section below. All three run on `MediaWorkQueue`, so they wait for a running scan. A range is `EarlierToday()` or `EarlierCustom(from, to)` (`domain/rules/earlier_window.dart`).

1. `earlierWindowBounds(range, now)` (pure): `now` read in local time; the start is local midnight of the "from" day (Today: today), the end is local midnight of the day after the "to" day, both built with `DateTime(y, m, d + n)` and returned in UTC. A "to" after today is clamped to today; "from" after "to", or after today, throws `ArgumentError` (before anything is read).
2. `bridge.accessReport()`. Not `full`: `countEarlier` and `countEarlierIn` return 0, `addEarlier` throws `StateError`. Nothing else is read.
3. `SettingsRepository.load().watchedFolders`. `PhoneStateRepository.volumeVersions()` once. For each mounted volume from `bridge.volumes()`: first `LibraryResync.catchUp(volume, recorded versions)` (the scan's and the verifier's rule: it records the volume's library version when none is recorded, so a rebuild right after the first clips came from here is noticed, and after a rebuild it matches the stored clips by fingerprint before their numbers are compared; M-3), then `bridge.videosShotSince(volume, folders, start)` (a `PlatformException` from either leaves that volume out); keep the rows with `shotInWindow(v, start, end, now:)` (usable, shot at or after the start and before the end); drop each row whose number `ClipsRepository.mediaStoreClipsHolding` reports held by a clip that is `sameVideo` with it (in To tag, Skipped or a reel). A clip holding the number of another video does not hide the row.
4. `countEarlier()` is `countEarlierIn(EarlierToday())`: how many rows are left. `countEarlierIn(range)` is the same for any range. Adds no clip; the only writes are `catchUp`'s from step 3 (a recorded version, or after a rebuild the matching of the stored clips).
5. `addEarlier(range)`: those rows. None → `[]`, nothing written. Otherwise `ZoneSync.sync()` (a failure is logged by type and the add goes on), then `ZoneRepository.all()` and `bridge.currentZoneId()`, `addCheckedClips` with the same check (new `clips` + `inbox` `pending` rows; a stored clip that is another video is detached). It writes nothing to the Shooting state (D-90). Returns the inserted clip ids: what `countEarlierIn` counted for that range, unless the phone changed in between.

6. **Opening the sheet:** `EarlierVideosController.build` calls `countEarlier()` (Today); it works with Shooting mode off. A failure is logged by type (never the message) and becomes an `AsyncError` state, which is not retried by itself.
7. **Counting dates:** `EarlierVideosController.countIn(range)` calls `countEarlierIn(range)`. A failure is logged by type and rethrown; the state is not touched.
8. **Add:** `EarlierVideosController.add(range)` calls `addEarlier(range)`, then `countEarlier()` again (Today's count now leaves out the added videos). A failure is logged by type and rethrown, so the sheet can say it did not work; if only the recount fails, the old count stays.
9. **Undo:** `EarlierVideosController.undo(ids)` runs `ClipsRepository.undoEarlierAdd(ids.toSet())` on `MediaWorkQueue` (so never beside a scan), then counts Today again. In one transaction `undoEarlierAdd` deletes the `inbox` row of each id that is still `pending` (not skipped) and in no reel, then runs the orphan sweep (D-56), and returns how many clips went (the ids are taken in batches of 500, all in the one transaction). A clip tagged or skipped since it was added stays; an empty set or a second undo deletes nothing. (`applyVerification` is not used: it only removes clips flagged missing, so Undo would do nothing.)

No baseline is read or written.

### Adding videos by hand (`lib/data/media/add_videos_service.dart`, `lib/domain/rules/browse_folders.dart`, D-113)

The folder browser (Task 5) and Manual mode (Task 6) call it through `addVideosServiceProvider`. A target is `AddToToTag()` or `AddToReel(reelId)`.

1. **The folder list:** `browseFolders(bridge.videoFolders())` (pure): drops folders with no videos and WhatsApp's Sent (last segment `Sent`, a segment before it starting `WhatsApp`, A to Z case ignored); names each by its last segment (`BrowseFolder.mainFolder`, `''`, for a volume root); gives folders that share a name their `parent` (the segment before the name; a root has none) and, when they span volumes, their `storage` (`FolderStorage.phone` for `primaryVolumeName`, `external_primary`; else `FolderStorage.other(n)`, `n` = 1, 2, … by sorted volume name when the shown folders are on two or more other volumes, else none; a volume's name is never shown, D-114); orders `DCIM/Camera` first, then by count, name, path and volume.
2. **Marks:** `marks(volume, mediaStoreIds, target)` runs `ClipsRepository.placesOf` (attached, not-missing MediaStore clips holding those numbers, with their `inbox` state and reels; batches of `idBatch`, all three reads in one read transaction, so a tag landing between them cannot show a clip in To tag and in a reel at once), then `addMarkFor` per clip: `inThisReel` (target reel it is in), else `inToTag` (pending), else `inAReel` (target To tag, in any reel). Not on the media queue.
3. **`addFromFolder(videos, target)`**, one `MediaWorkQueue` job: `bridge.accessReport()` not `full` → `StateError`, nothing read or written. `bridge.volumes()`; a video that is not `isUsable` or whose volume is not mounted is `notKept`; none left → `AddResult(0, 0, notKept)`, nothing more read or written. For each volume of the rest, `LibraryResync.catchUp` (records the version, or matches the stored clips again after a rebuild). `ZoneSync.sync()` (a failure is logged by type), `ZoneRepository.all()`, `bridge.currentZoneId()`; each video becomes `clipFromVideo` (the scanner's own function); then step 5 with `sameVideo` against the folder row.
4. **`addPicked(videos, target)`**, one queue job, any access: `persisted == false` is `notKept` (none left → nothing read or written); the rest become `newClipFromPicked` (date `shotAtFrom(takenMs)`, else now; no type `video/*`; missing facts `0` or `''`), zone as in step 3; step 5 with every known address taken as the same video.
5. **`ClipsRepository.addByHand(clips, reelId:, sameVideo:)`**, one transaction, in two named steps (`_placeByHand`: read where the known clips are, decide each one in order, write the detaches and the inserts with their places; `_settleByHand`: Skipped back to `pending`, new links, inbox rows out, the reel's `updatedAt`, the sweep of the detached; then `_handAddRecord` builds the Undo record): the reel must exist (`StateError`). Known clips, their `inbox` rows and links are read in batches. Per unique identity: a known MediaStore clip that is not `sameVideo` is detached (`-id`, missing); new (or placeless) → inserted with a `pending` row (To tag) or a link (reel); To tag: skipped → `pending` (`addedAt` kept), pending or in a reel → `alreadyThere`; reel: in it or pending → `alreadyThere`, skipped → linked and its row deleted, only in other reels → linked. The reel's `updatedAt` is set when a link was added; detached clips only in a list are then swept (`_removeMissing`). Returns `HandAdd(added, alreadyThere, undo, detached)`; the service returns `AddResult(added, alreadyThere, notKept)`.
6. **`undo(result)`**: nothing to undo → returns. Else one queue job running `ClipsRepository.undoHandAdd`, one transaction: `UndoWriter.applyUndo` on the `InboxUndo` (back to Skipped, only if still pending with the same `addedAt`) or `AddUndo` (Skipped rows back, links of existing clips removed when the clip has another place); the add's new clips lose their `pending` row (same `addedAt`, no link) or their link (same `addedAt`); the reel's `updatedAt` goes back if unchanged; the orphan sweep. The record is marked undone after the commit, so a second call returns at once and a failed one can be retried.

Logs (`addVideos`, `clips`): counts, the target kind, reel ids and error types only (D-10).

**In tests:** `test/domain/rules/browse_folders_test.dart`; `test/data/media/add_videos_service_test.dart` on `MediaHarness` (which takes a `database:` such as `loggedTestDatabase` for the batching and rollback tests) and `FakeMediaBridge`; the repository's own cases, including the D-57 sweep race, in `test/data/repositories/clips_repository_test.dart` (`addByHand`).

### The folder browser (`lib/features/add_videos/`, `lib/router/`, Phase 7 Task 5, D-114)

**Opening** (`views/open_add_videos.dart › openAddVideos(context, ref, target)`): To tag's app bar **Add videos** (`library_add`, label "Add videos") and its "Nothing to tag" state, and the reel page's app bar button and its "No clips yet" state, call it through their own `OpenOnce` (a double tap opens one list). It reads, before anything awaits, the bridge, `addVideosServiceProvider`, `reelsRepositoryProvider`, `ReelSnacks.of(context)`, the words and `GoRouter.of(context)`; then `bridge.accessReport()` (a throw is logged by type and counts as no access). Not `full` (limited, denied, or a read that failed) → Manual mode, "The Manual mode sheet" below. Full → (after the same `context.mounted` check, final review M-7: a Back during the slow read opens nothing) `router.push<AddResult>(Routes.addVideosTo(reelId:))` and waits; `pushing` is called just after the push is made. `null` (Back, or the screens left) → nothing. Else the result goes to `announce`, the one place that says what an add did, whichever route it came from (D-115): `added == 0` → "Already added" when `alreadyThere > 0`, and a line "Couldn't add {n} videos." (`addVideosNotKept`, plural) when `notKept > 0`; both lines are joined with a newline in one plain snack, and when both counts are zero the snack is "Already added". `added > 0` → "{n} videos added to {target}" ({target}: "To tag", or the reel's name read now; a reel gone meanwhile shows nothing; a name that cannot be read, a throw logged by type, is "this reel" and the snack and Undo still show), then, when `notKept > 0`, the "Couldn't add {n} videos." line after a newline, in one snack with **Undo** → `AddVideosService.undo(result)` through `ReelSnacks.showUndoAll(message, [], undo:)` on the root messenger, so it works after switching tabs; a failed Undo says "Couldn't undo. Try again.". It is one snack because a new snack hides the one showing, which would take **Undo** away.

### The Manual mode sheet (`views/manual_pick_sheet.dart`, Phase 7 Task 6, D-115)

`openAddVideos` without full access (after a `context.mounted` check: the caller may have gone during a slow access read) calls `showManualPickSheet(context, target:, onOutcome:)` (a `TtSheet` on the root navigator, titled "Pick videos yourself"). It has the line "TakeTray can see only some of your videos (Manual mode), so it opens Android's photo picker.", a `TtCallout` (`info`) "The photo picker doesn't show folder counts, and it can't mark videos you've already added.", a large filled **Open photo picker** and a text **Allow full access instead** (both full width in the footer; 48 dp or more).

**Open photo picker** → `OpenOnce.openSlow` → `_pick()`: reads the bridge, `addVideosServiceProvider`, the target, the callback and the navigator first (the sheet may be gone by the end); `_picking = true` (both buttons off); `bridge.pickVideos()`:
- `[]` (cancelled): logged, nothing else; the sheet closes, no outcome, no snack.
- some videos: `AddVideosService.addPicked(picked, target)` (any access, one job on the media queue) → `PickedAdded(result)` → the page's `announce` (see "Opening"); the sheet closes.
- `PlatformException` code `busy` (a picker is open already): logged, no outcome, the sheet stays and the buttons come back on.
- any other throw, from the pick or the add (the target reel gone is a `StateError` from the add): logged by type (and the picker's error code), `PickFailed` → "Couldn't add videos. Try again."; the sheet closes.

The outcome callback runs even when the sheet was dragged away meanwhile, so a pick is never lost. **Nothing here runs `PickedGrants.sweep`:** the clean-up runs only on the first frame of an app start, so no grant sits between its pick and its clip row (D-112, D-115). **Allow full access instead** → closes the sheet, then `MediaAccessController.allow()` (not awaited). Logs (`manualPick`): counts and error types only.

**The reel page** shows `MediaAccessBanner` as its first sliver, so a user who comes back to it after losing access (the browser closes both screens, D-114) reads "TakeTray can't see your videos" with **Allow**; in Manual mode it says "Manual mode" with **Allow full access**. To tag and the Reels tab already show it.

**Routes** (`app_router.dart › addVideosRoute()`, a top-level `GoRoute`, so both screens are on the root navigator, full screen above the shell): `/add-videos` (`?reel=<id>` for a reel) → `AddFoldersScreen(target)`; child `folder` → `/add-videos/folder?volume=<v>&path=<p>[&reel=<id>]` (`Routes.addVideosFolderOf`, `Uri`-encoded) → `AddGridScreen(target, volumeName, relativePath)` (no `path` is the root). A `reel` that is not a positive whole number, or a grid location with no or an empty `volume`, is redirected to `/reels` and logged without the location (it can hold a path). Tests use the same function through `pumpScreen(addVideos: true)`.

**Leaving** (`views/add_videos_flow.dart`): both screens call `leaveWhenAddVideosEnds(ref, target, leave)` in `build`: `ref.listen(mediaAccessControllerProvider)` (a level other than `full` → `leave` in a microtask) and, for a reel, `ref.listen(reelByIdProvider(id))` (a value of `null` → `leave`). The grid's `leave` pops with the sentinel `addVideosLeave` (while an add runs it only notes it: an `AddDone` then pops with its result, an `AddFailed` or `AddTargetGone` with the sentinel); the folder list's `leave` pops only while it is the current route (`ModalRoute.isCurrent`), and when the grid hands it `addVideosLeave` or an `AddResult` it pops with `null` or that result. So access lost or the reel deleted closes both screens, back to the page that opened them (To tag's and the reel page's access banner then explain), and an add returns its result to `openAddVideos`.

**The folder list** (`views/add_folders_screen.dart`, `view_models/add_folders_controller.dart`):
1. `AddFoldersController.build` → `browseFolders(bridge.videoFolders())` (logged: the count only). An error → `TtEmptyState(folder)` "Couldn't load your folders" / "Your videos are safe in your gallery." / **Try again** (`retry`, `invalidateSelf`). Loading → skeleton rows. An empty list → "No videos yet" / "Videos saved on your phone will show up here.".
2. `TtAppBar` "Add videos" with "to To tag" or "to {reel}" (`addVideosTargetText`, from `reelByIdProvider`; none until the name is known) and **Back** (`pop`).
3. The tip: `FolderTip` reads `PhoneStateRepository.folderTipSeen()` (key `folderTipSeen`, phone state: never exported, an import forgets it); only a known `false` shows the `TtCallout` (`tips_and_updates`) "Keep friends' clips sharp" / "Ask friends to send videos as HD or by Quick Share to keep full quality." with a text **Got it** → `dismiss()`: the state becomes `true` at once, then `markFolderTipSeen()` (a failure is logged by type).
4. The rows: one `SliverList.builder` of `TtListRow(tall)` on grouped surfaces (`TtMetrics.groupedRows*` radii and gap): leading `_FolderPreview` (56 dp, `tt.radii.s`, `surfaceContainerHigh`), title = the name or "Main folder", subtitle = "{n} videos" joined with " · " (`addFoldersJoin`) to the `parent` and to `storageText` ("Phone", "Other storage", "Other storage {n}"), trailing chevron. Only built rows exist, so only their previews are read.
5. `_FolderPreview` watches `folderPreviewProvider(volume, path)` = `AddFoldersController.previewOf(volume, path)`, which reads `videosInFolder(volume, path, 4, 0)` once and **keeps the answer for the life of the folder list** (a row scrolled away and back reads nothing; a failed read is dropped so its row asks again; `refresh()` forgets them all; they go with the controller when the screen closes), mapped to `FolderVideo`s: up to four `Image(clipThumbnail(clip: video.picture, logicalSize: 56, cache:))`, two to a row (one row for one or two pictures, two rows for three or four, like the prototype's CSS grid); an error, or an empty page, shows `Symbols.folder_rounded` (20 dp). Hidden from screen readers.
6. A tap → `OpenOnce.open(push(Routes.addVideosFolderOf(volume, path, reelId:)))`; the exit is handled as in "Leaving".
7. `didChangeAppLifecycleState(resumed)` → `AddFoldersController.refresh()` (keeps the list on a failure) and `ref.invalidate(folderPreviewProvider)`.

**The grid** (`views/add_grid_screen.dart`, `view_models/add_grid_controller.dart`, Review Focus 1):
1. `AddGridController(target, volumeName, relativePath).build` reads one page, `videosInFolder(volume, path, addGridPageSize = 200, 0)`, makes each row a `FolderVideo` (`shotAt` by the scan's rule with one `now` per read; `picture`: a never-stored `Clip` with the identity and fingerprint `clipFromVideo` will give it, so the thumbnail cache file is shared with the later clip), dropping a repeated id; then `AddVideosService.marks(volume, ids, target)` (a throw → no marks, logged by type). State: `videos`, `marks`, `selected` (ordered set of ids), `hasMore` (the last page was full), `rowsRead` (the next offset), `loadingMore`, `moreFailed`, `adding`. An error → "Couldn't load these videos" with **Try again** (`retry`). Loading → nine skeleton tiles. No videos → "No videos in this folder" / "Videos saved here will show up when you come back.".
2. The rows: `_rowsFor(videos)` (recomputed only when the list changes): a `TtDayHeader(toTagDayText(day))` for each run of videos on one local day (newest first, as the phone sorts; an odd date that comes back gets a header again), then the run three to a row, all in one `SliverList.builder`. A tile: `TtClipTile(key: 'add-video-<id>', thumbnail: clipThumbnail(video.picture, tile height), timeText: timeOfDayText(shotAt), durationText, horizontal)`; a marked one has `mark` ("In this reel" `check_circle`, "In To tag" `sell`, "In a reel" `video_library`), `selected: null` and a tap that shows "Already added" (root messenger); an unmarked one has `selected` and a tap → `toggle(id)`. Semantics: "Clip, {time}, {duration}" or "Clip, {time}, {duration}, {mark}".
3. **Paging:** building a row within `_loadAheadRows` (20) of the end, while `hasMore` and not `loadingMore` or `moreFailed`, asks `loadMore()` after the frame. `loadMore` returns the running read when there is one (same epoch); reads `videosInFolder(…, 200, rowsRead)`, drops ids already shown, reads their marks, then appends (unless a refresh started since: the `_epoch` check drops the page). A failure → `moreFailed`: "Couldn't load more videos." with **Try again** (`loadMore`) at the end; while loading, three skeleton tiles there. The choice is never touched by paging.
4. `toggle(id)` does nothing (returns false) for a marked id, an id not shown, or while adding.
5. The bottom bar (`Scaffold.bottomNavigationBar`, so snacks sit above it; `surfaceContainer`, an `outlineVariant` top line): "No videos picked" / "Videos already added are marked" and a disabled **Add**, or "{n} videos picked" / "{m:ss} · added in shooting order" (`formatClipDuration(pickedDurationMs)`) and **Add {n}** (live region). **Add** is off while `adding`.
6. `_add()` → `add()`: null when nothing is picked or an add runs (so two taps in one frame add once); sets `adding` (the choice and Back freeze: `PopScope(canPop: !adding)`, Back button off); `addFromFolder(picked oldest first, target)`. `added + alreadyThere > 0` → `AddDone(result)` (stays frozen) and the grid pops with the result. Nothing kept → `AddFailed`. A throw → `AddTargetGone` when the target reel no longer exists (`getById`), else `AddFailed`; both unfreeze and keep the choice. On `AddFailed` the grid shows "Couldn't add videos. Try again." and runs `MediaAccessController.refresh()` (a lost access then closes the screens) and `refresh()` (a removed card empties the grid). Once the screen has left (access lost or the reel deleted while the add ran), nothing is shown.
7. `didChangeAppLifecycleState(resumed)` → `refresh()` (not while adding; a failed first page is read again): reads pages from 0 until `max(rowsRead, 200)` rows or a short page, reads the marks, and keeps in the choice only ids that are still shown and unmarked. A removed volume gives an empty page (D-112), so the grid shows its empty state and nothing stays picked.

Logs (`addVideos`, `addFolders`, `addGrid`): counts and error types only, never a folder, a name or a reel's name (D-10).

**In tests:** `test/features/add_videos/` on `AddHarness` (`add_videos_support.dart`: a `VmHarness` on a `FolderBridge` that records `videosInFolder` calls, a `FakeAccess` and a `CountingAddVideos` that counts, holds or fails adds, and fails marks): `views/add_folders_screen_test.dart`, `views/add_grid_screen_test.dart` (paging over 450 videos: three pages), `views/add_videos_entry_test.dart` (To tag, the reel page, and the whole app with Undo after switching tabs; `AddHarness.makeReelsUnreadable` makes `getById` throw), `view_models/add_grid_controller_test.dart`; the routes in `test/router/app_router_test.dart`; `views/manual_pick_sheet_test.dart` (the Manual mode sheet and its picks, on `CountingAddVideos.pickedAdds` and `pickedVideo`); screenshots, with the Manual mode sheet, in `views/add_videos_golden_test.dart` (macOS only).

### Design-system pieces for editors (`packages/taketray_ui/lib/src/components/`, Phase 8 Task 8, D-125)

Five shared widgets, built to `proto.css` and `screens.js`, with no wording of their own (every text is a parameter, so the screens of Tasks 9 to 13 pass their own l10n strings). Their numbers are in `TtMetrics` (the "Storage bar", "Illustrated steps", "Status layout" and "App icon" sections); the style check passes without exceptions.

1. `storage_bar.dart › TtStorageBar`: `.enough(needFraction, needLabel, freeAfterLabel, semanticLabel)` and `.short(haveFraction, haveLabel, shortLabel, semanticLabel)`. A 14 dp track (`surfaceContainerHighest`, 7 dp corners, clipped) over a two-item legend (`Wrap`, 14 dp across, 6 dp down, swatches 10 dp with 3 dp corners).
   1. `enough`: one primary segment, `max(needFraction, 0.04)` of the track, never more than all of it (`0.04` is `TtMetrics.storageMinNeedFraction`, from the prototype's `Math.max(4, ...)`). Legend: a primary swatch with `needLabel`, a track-coloured swatch with a 1 dp `outlineVariant` outline with `freeAfterLabel`.
   2. `short`: the bar's scale is what the copies need. `haveFraction` (an `assert`: `0 <= f < 1`, so NaN, infinity, a negative and 1 or more are refused in debug; in release `internal/unit_share.dart › unitShare` makes any such share safe) is the solid `outline` part; the rest is a `HatchPainter` (`internal/hatch_painter.dart`, `CustomPaint`) in `error`, 6 dp stripes at 45 degrees, the second stripe at 70 % (the CSS `repeating-linear-gradient`). Legend: an `outline` swatch with `haveLabel`, an `error` swatch with `shortLabel`. Task 10 passes `clamp((free - copyReserveBytes) / need, 0, 1)` capped below 1 only when the space check says "short".
   3. The bar and legend are one semantics node reading `semanticLabel` (`ExcludeSemantics` under a `Semantics(container: true, label:)`), so a screen reader never hears the legend twice. Keys `tt-storage-track / -need / -have / -short` are for tests.
2. `bottom_bar.dart › TtBottomBar(title, subtitle, action)`: `surfaceContainer`, a 1 dp `outlineVariant` line on its top edge, `SafeArea(top: false)`, 16 dp at the sides and 12 dp above and below, a 12 dp gap. The two lines are one live region (the count is read when it changes) in title-small and body-small, wrap at large text and grow the bar; `action` keeps its own size (a `TtButton` is 48 dp). The Add videos grid (`features/add_videos/views/add_grid_screen.dart › _BottomBar`) now builds a `TtBottomBar` and keeps its keys; its macOS goldens are unchanged.
3. `illustrated_steps.dart › TtIllustratedSteps(steps)` with `TtIllustratedStep.newProject / .folders / .tiles` (each `number`, `title`, `body`). A step is a 112 x 132 dp picture, 14 dp, then number (label-medium, primary), title (title-small) and body (body-medium); steps are 12 dp apart. The text is one merged semantics node. The picture is a drawing: it is inside `MediaQuery.withNoTextScaling` so it keeps its size at large text, and hidden from semantics except in `.folders`.
   1. `.newProject`: two outline-variant bars (60 % and 40 % of the 96 dp inside) and a 56 dp `onSurface` plus button with a 4 dp `tertiaryContainer` ring, centred in the rest.
   2. `.folders(folders, highlighted)`: one to four 20 dp rows (an `assert` for the count and for `highlighted` being one of them); the highlighted row is `tertiaryContainer` with a filled folder icon and its name is announced as **selected** (`Semantics(selected: true)`); a long name is cut on one line.
   3. `.tiles(thumbnails)`: the first six of any number of 3:4 tiles (none leaves the plain picture), three to a row, 3 dp apart, each with a 12 dp primary number badge 1..n; a thumbnail that fails to load leaves its `outlineVariant` tile and the badge.
4. `status_layout.dart › TtStatusLayout(icon, tone, title, body?, actions)` and `TtStatusTone { primary, error, warning, success }`: a `Column` that fills its height. The top part is a scroll view that centres a 48 dp icon (`primary`, `error`, `tertiary`, `TtColors.success`; hidden from semantics), the title (headline-small, a heading) and the optional body, 12 dp apart with 32 dp at the sides; a plain `Text` body gets body-medium in `onSurfaceVariant`, centred (`DefaultTextStyle.merge`), any other widget is placed as given. At large text the block scrolls and the actions stay in view. In a parent with no height limit (a list) the block is as tall as its content and the actions follow it. The actions are under it, `SafeArea(top: false)`, 16 dp at the sides and bottom, 12 dp above, 8 dp apart, as wide as the layout.
5. `app_icon.dart › TtAppIcon(fallbackLetters, png?)`: 40 dp, 12 dp corners. With bytes it is an `Image.memory` (cover, clipped); with none, empty bytes, or bytes `Image` cannot decode (`errorBuilder`) it is an `surfaceContainerHighest` tile with the letters in bold `onSurfaceVariant` (12 dp, never scaled with the text size, shrunk to fit). It is hidden from semantics: the editor's name is beside it.

Tests: `packages/taketray_ui/test/components/{storage_bar,bottom_bar,illustrated_steps,status_layout,app_icon}_test.dart` and `_golden_test.dart` (light and dark, 1x and 2x; `goldens/macos` made on the Mac, `goldens/ci` by the "update goldens" workflow, see "Updating CI goldens"). The Widgetbook has a use case for each (`widgetbook/lib/components/`), and `catalog_coverage_test.dart` stays green.

### App start and resume (`lib/app/media_lifecycle.dart`, `lib/features/*/view_models/`, D-75)

`MediaLifecycle` is a widget in `TakeTrayApp` above the router. It has an `AppLifecycleListener` and shows its child unchanged.

1. **First frame** (`addPostFrameCallback`): starts the prune (step 6), queues the picked grants clean-up on the media queue (`PickedGrants.sweep`, Phase 7; before the run's scan, once per app start, guarded; see "Picked clips: pictures, checks and the grant clean-up") and a run (steps 2 to 5).
1b. **Run, step 0: the copy clean-up hook.** `ref.read(editSessionServiceProvider)` (guarded), so the editing service exists and has handed `ClipsRepository` and `ReelsRepository` their `beforeLeave` hook before the scan, whose re-attach can merge a duplicate out of a reel (D-123, R8).
2. **Run, step 1: zone sync.** `ZoneSync.sync()`. This stays even though the scan syncs too: with Shooting mode off the scan does nothing, and the timeline must still learn the zone changes.
3. **Run, step 2: access.** `MediaAccessController.refresh()`: reads `bridge.accessReport()`; seeing `full` for the first time stores `mediaAccessWasFull`; the new `MediaAccessView(level, banner, canAskAgain)` is shown. The banner (`bannerFor`): full → `none`; limited → `manual`; denied with `accessWasFull` → `lost`; denied without it → `manual`. Several calls at once: only the last one to start writes the state. If the very first read fails the state is an `AsyncError` until a later `refresh()` or `allow()` succeeds; a first read that fails after one of those already published a view hands that view back instead of replacing it.
3b. **Run, step 2b: notification permission.** `NotificationAccessController.refresh()` (D-107): reads `BackgroundBridge.notificationAccess()` and shows it, so the Notifications row in Settings is right when the user comes back from the phone's settings. Guarded like every step.
4. **Run, step 3: scan.** `MediaScanner.scan()` (see "Finding new clips"). Its gate applies a Still shooting? button tapped in the shade first (D-106), so the run has no shade step of its own (fix round 2: a separate apply was a second try of the same end).
4b. **Run, step 3b: the copy job** (D-123), whatever the scan returned. On the first run of the mount (the app's start): `ref.read(copyJobControllerProvider)` (so the copy job is followed from now on), then `EditSessionService.recoverAtStart()`, once (see "Editing a reel" › "After Android stopped TakeTray"). On every later run (a resume): `CopyJobController.onResume()`, which only reads the engine's job again (`CopyBridge.current()`) and records what it holds; never a second recovery.
5. **Run, step 4: after a scan that read every volume** (`ScanDone` with no failed volumes; a capped end from the shade counts; not after `ScanShootingOff`, `ScanNoAccess`, `ScanDeferred`, a failed volume, which may still hold clips the job counts, or a scan that threw): `BackgroundSync.flush()`, then `BackgroundBridge.clearNewClipsNotification()` (D-106). The flush writes the moved baselines into the phone's copy first, so a new-clips job that runs after the clear counts with them, not the old ones, and cannot post the clips just scanned again. A failed write is logged by the flush, and the clear still runs.
6. **Prune, once per app start (once per mount of `MediaLifecycle`):** awaits `thumbnailCacheProvider.future`, then `ThumbnailCache.prune()`. Not repeated on resume.
7. **Every step is guarded** (`core/guarded.dart › guarded`): an error of any kind is logged as `<step> failed: <ErrorType>` (never the message or a stack trace, which may hold a path) and the next step still runs. Nothing reaches the UI as an uncaught error; the UI never waits for a run.
8. **Resume** (`AppLifecycleListener.onResume`): the same run, without the prune or the grant clean-up.
9. **Runs never overlap.** A start or resume while a run is going sets a flag; when the run ends, exactly one more run follows, however many triggers came. The scanner's own coalescing (D-72) and the media queue stay in force underneath.

**Other places that start media work (all guarded, none waited for by the UI):**
- `ToTagController.build` (when To tag is opened) and `onResume()`: `scan()`, then `verify` of the clips waiting in To tag (`watchInbox(pending).first`). The two are independent: a failed scan does not skip the verify. The list is the database's own (`watchInbox(pending)`) whatever happens.
- `MediaAccessController.allow()`: reads a fresh `accessReport()`. Full already → shows it, scans. `canAskAgain` or limited → `requestAccess()`; a full answer stores `mediaAccessWasFull` and scans; limited or denied is only shown. Otherwise `openAppSettings()`. A second request while one waits (`busy`) is logged and changes nothing.
- `ShootingModeController.setOn(true)`: calls run one after another in the order made, so the last call wins. `askIfDenied()` (a request only if denied and `canAskAgain`); `NotificationAccessController.askOnce()` (D-107: the notification question, once ever, see "Notification permission"); baselines `{volume: VolumeBaseline(version, generation)}` of the mounted volumes (`version != null`) from `bridge.volumes()`, or `{}` if that throws; `turnShootingOn(baselines)` (already on: the first session is kept); then a scan. `setOn(false)` calls `turnShootingOff()` and leaves To tag alone. `openCamera()` returns `bridge.openVideoCamera()`, or false on a failure.

### To tag, the Shooting mode card and Add earlier videos (`lib/features/{tagging,shooting_mode,media_access}/views/`, D-76)

**Opening To tag** (`tagging/views/to_tag_screen.dart › ToTagScreen`):
1. The first visit to the To tag tab builds `ToTagScreen`; `build` watches `toTagControllerProvider`, `toTagTimesProvider` (see "The time where a clip was shot"), `shootingModeControllerProvider`, `mediaAccessControllerProvider` and `thumbnailCacheProvider`. Watching `toTagControllerProvider` builds `ToTagController`, which scans and then verifies the waiting clips, once (D-75, "App start and resume").
2. The shell keeps the tab built. `didChangeDependencies` reads `TickerMode.valuesOf(context).enabled` (go_router turns it off for a hidden tab); when it goes from off to on, `ToTagController.onResume()` runs (scan, then verify). `didChangeAppLifecycleState(resumed)` does the same while the tab is showing. The very first build does not call `onResume()`.
3. Body, top to bottom (amended by D-98): `TtAppBar(large)` with **Add videos** (a circle `TtIconButton`, `library_add`, label "Add videos": `openAddVideos(AddToToTag())` through an `OpenOnce`, "The folder browser") and a `TtMenuButton` (⋮, label "More options") holding **Add earlier videos** (only when `access?.level == full`), **Select clips** and **Skip all** (only while clips wait) and **Skipped ({n})** (only when `skippedCountProvider` is above 0); with nothing to put in it there is no button. Then `MediaAccessBanner`; the strip; then the error state (`clips.hasError`), else, while the list or the Shooting state is loading (`clips.isLoading || scenes == null || shooting.isLoading`; the times never hold it back) six `TtClipTileSkeleton`s, else the empty state (no clips) or the scenes; at the end `TtNavPill.reservedHeight` of room. **Start tagging** (a `TtFab` at `TtNavPill.fabBottom`, lifted over a snack by itself, D-82) sits over the list while clips wait, nothing is chosen and no tag is leaving.
4. The strip: Shooting mode on → `TtStatStrip` with `TtRecDot`, "Shooting since {time}" and a text **Open camera** (`openCameraOrSay`: `ShootingModeController.openCamera`, and a snack when it cannot open, see "Home"). Off with clips waiting → muted strip, `videocam_off`, "Shooting mode off", **Turn on** (`setOn(true)`). Off with none → no strip. A shooting state that failed to load counts as off.
5. The empty state: on → `TtEmptyState(caught)` "All caught up" with a tonal **Open camera**; off → `TtEmptyState(shootOff)` "Nothing to tag" with **Turn on Shooting mode** (`setOn(true)`) and a text **Add videos** (as the app bar's). Under either, a text **Skipped ({n})** when anything is skipped (`context.push(Routes.skipped)`). **The error state** (D-98): `TtEmptyState` with the folder picture, "Couldn't load your clips", "Your videos are safe in your gallery." and a tonal **Try again**, which calls `ToTagController.retry()` (`ref.invalidateSelf()`: the list is read again, with a scan and a verify, and the skeleton shows meanwhile).
6. The scene list (D-98): `toTagScenesProvider` (`groupIntoScenes(clips, times)`, D-95; the phone's time for a clip whose time is not known yet, so it never waits) gives the scenes. `_rows` turns them into one flat list for a single `SliverList.builder`: a `TtDayHeader` (`toTagDayText`: "Today · Wed, Oct 28", "Yesterday · …", or the plain date; **only when the scenes span two days or more**, one for each day), a `TtSceneHeader` (`timeRangeText(scene.start, scene.end, ...)` over "{n} clips · {m:ss}", with **Tag scene**, whose semantics are "Tag scene {range}, {n} clips"), then the scene's tiles three to a row (`views/clip_tile_row.dart › ClipTileRow`, shared with Skipped; 300 clips over 60 scenes build a screenful). A tile is `TtClipTile(thumbnail: clipThumbnail(...), timeText:` the time where it was shot (`wallClockTimeText(times[clip.id].wallClock, ...)`, D-83; `timeOfDayText(dateTakenUtc, ...)`, the phone's time, while the times load, when the map has no entry for the clip yet, or when the times failed; the tile switches when the answer arrives)`, durationText: formatClipDuration(...), semanticLabel: "Clip, {time}, {duration}")`; a clip with `missing == true` is `TtMissingClipTile("Missing from phone")`. The summary row above them: "{n} clips · {s} scenes · {mins}" (`minutesText`) and, when anything is skipped, **Skipped ({n})** (opened through its own `OpenOnce`, so a double tap pushes one page). While loading: the prototype's skeleton (a summary line, then two scenes of a header line over six skeleton tiles). A tile tap: `OpenOnce.open(() => context.push(Routes.tagFrom(clip.id)))`; **Start tagging**: the same with the first clip in shooting order that is not missing.

**Tag scene / Tag** (`_tag(ids)`, D-98): `_busy` makes a second tap do nothing. The screen keeps the scenes as they are (`_held`), then `pickAndPerform(mode: ReelPickerMode.tag, clipIds: ids, fromReelId: null, ...)`: the picker lists every reel (nothing is excluded), titled "Tag {n} clips" over "Pick one or more reels.", confirm "Tag {n} clips" (none or one reel picked) or "Tag into {k} reels"; confirm calls `ReelClipActions.addToReels` (one `addClipsToReels` transaction), then `LastTags.set(pickedReelIds)` (so the player's **Same as previous** follows it); the sheet closes; the snack "{n} clips added to {names}" ({n}: the clips that got a new link, from the `AddUndo` records) (`joinedNamesText`: "A", "A and B", "A, B and {k} more") with **Undo** (`ReelsRepository.undoAll`, then `LastTags.undo`, which puts the old list back unless another tag has been remembered since). **Scene tagging motion:** once the sheet has closed, each tagged tile fades (and, when its whole row goes, the row folds up) over 250 ms, `TtMotion.stagger` (30 ms) after the one before it (the whole run squeezed into 350 ms for a long list); under "remove animations" they only fade, together, in 150 ms, and no row folds. The list is not tappable meanwhile; then `_held` is dropped and the list is the database's again. A cancelled picker plays nothing.

**Choosing** (`ToTagSelection`, D-98, as the reel page's, D-88): a long press calls `start(id)`, **Select clips** `begin()`; the app bar is `TtAppBar(selection: true, liveRegion: true)` with close, "{n} selected", **Skip** and **Tag** (icon buttons named "Skip" and "Tag" above `TtMetrics.appBarMaxTextScale`, so the count stays whole); each scene header reads **Select all** / **Unselect** (`toggleScene`; announced "Select all, {range}, {n} clips" / "Unselect, …"); a tap toggles; un-choosing the last clip ends choosing. A missing clip cannot be chosen. The selection follows `toTagControllerProvider`: a chosen clip that leaves To tag (a verify, a scan, the player, a skip) is un-chosen, and choosing with 0 chosen ends when the list empties. `PopScope(canPop: !selecting)`: the system back clears the choice before it leaves. **Skip** (`_skipChosen`): `ClipsRepository.skipClips(ids)` → "{n} clips skipped" (n = rows that moved) with **Undo** (`undoAll` of the `InboxUndo`); a failure shows "Something went wrong. Try again." and keeps the choice.

**Skip all** (`_skipAll`, D-98): the ⋮ item opens `showSkipAllDialog` ("Skip all {n} clips?", "They move to Skipped, and you can restore them any time.", the green "Your videos stay in your gallery.", **Cancel** / **Skip all**); only **Skip all** goes on: `skipClips(ids)` of the clips the dialog counted when it opened (one transaction; a clip that arrived meanwhile stays waiting) → "{n} clips skipped" ({n}: the rows that moved) with **Undo**. **Cancel**, a tap outside or Back changes nothing.

**Skipped** (D-99) opens `Routes.skipped` (`/to-tag/skipped`, inside the To tag tab, nav pill kept): `features/tagging/views/skipped_screen.dart › SkippedScreen`.
- **Opening.** `build` watches `skippedControllerProvider` (`SkippedController`, `view_models/skipped_controller.dart`): the database's `watchInbox(skipped)` **reversed (newest first)**. Building it also starts one verify, `guarded(...)`: `MediaVerifier.verify(watchInbox(skipped).first)` on the shared media queue (D-72), so a skipped clip whose video is gone (and that is in no reel) leaves the list; an error is logged by type (`The verify failed: PlatformException`), never stops the list. **No scan**: a scan adds to To tag, not here.
- **Days.** `skippedDaysProvider` = `groupIntoDays(clips, times)` (`domain/rules/skipped_days.dart`): clips sorted by UTC instant, newest first (then higher id); each clip's day is its date where it was shot (`skippedTimesProvider` → `ClipTimeService.localTimes`, D-83; the phone's time while that is read or if it failed, so the list never waits); one entry per date (clips from two zones on one date share a header), **newest day first, newest clip first inside a day** (the prototype sorts the ids newest first and then cuts days, `q.days`). `null` until the list is read.
- **Screen.** `TtAppBar` "Skipped" with **Back** (`pop`, or `go(Routes.toTag)` when nothing can pop) and **Select** (only when something is skipped); `TtInfoBar` "Skipped clips are still in your gallery. Restore any you want to tag."; one lazy `SliverList.builder` of rows: a `TtDayHeader(toTagDayText(day), trailing: "{n} clips")` for each day (always, also with one day) and its tiles three to a row (`ClipTileRow`, as To tag's; the time where it was shot, the length; semantics "Skipped clip, {time}, {duration}. Restores it to To tag", "Clip, …" while choosing; a flagged-missing clip is `TtMissingClipTile` with **Remove**, never restored or chosen; see "Remove on a missing tile" below). Loading: skeleton tiles. Nothing skipped: `TtEmptyState(skipped)` "No skipped clips" / "Clips you skip in To tag show up here, in case you change your mind.", no Select, no note. Failed stream: To tag's "Couldn't load your clips" with **Try again** (`SkippedController.retry`).
- **Restoring** (`_restore(ids, single:)`): ids already being restored (`_restoring`) are left out, so a double tap on a tile or on **Restore** writes once. The messenger (`ReelSnacks.of`) and both notifiers are looked up before the write. `SkippedController.restore(ids)` → `ClipsRepository.restoreClips(ids)` (one transaction, an `InboxUndo`, D-94). A record that moved rows shows the snack on the **root messenger** with **Undo** (`ReelSnacks.showUndoAll`, which replaces the snack showing, so only the latest offers Undo; the `InboxUndo` is held by the snack, so Undo works after the screen has gone): a tile tap "Restored to To tag", the selection's **Restore** "{n} clips restored to To tag" (ICU plural, `n` = rows that moved). Nothing moved (the clip was restored or tagged elsewhere meanwhile): no snack. A failure: "Something went wrong. Try again." and the choice stays. Only the selection's **Restore** ends choosing; a tile tap's restore leaves alone a choice begun while it wrote. The restored clips are in To tag at their place in shooting order (`addedAt` kept); **Undo** puts back only rows still as the restore left them (D-94).
- **Choosing** (`SkippedSelection`, `view_models/skipped_selection.dart`, as To tag's): **Select** → `begin()` (0 chosen), a long press → `start(id)`, a tap while choosing → `toggle(id)`; un-choosing the last ends choosing. It follows `skippedControllerProvider`: a chosen clip that leaves Skipped is un-chosen, an emptied choice ends, and **when the list empties choosing ends** even with nothing chosen. `PopScope(canPop: !selecting)`: the system back clears the choice first. Selection bar: `TtAppBar(selection: true, liveRegion: true)` with close, "{n} selected" and **Restore** (off until a clip is chosen). **At text above 1.5x** (`TtMetrics.appBarMaxTextScale`, the size the bar draws its title at) **Restore is an icon button with the semantic label "Restore"**, so "{n} selected" is never cut short.
- **Privacy** (D-10): the logs hold counts and error types only.
7. `{time}` is `shooting_mode/views/shooting_time_text.dart › shootingTimeText(since, now, locale, use24Hour)`: both in local time; same day → `timeOfDayText`, otherwise `DateFormat.MMMd` + the time joined by ", ". `timeOfDayText(time, locale, use24Hour)` is `DateFormat.Hm` ("09:41") when the phone is set to 24-hour time, else `DateFormat.jm` ("9:41 AM"). `use24Hour` is `MediaQuery.alwaysUse24HourFormatOf(context)`, read in `build` by the card, the To tag strip and the grid (so a change redraws them); the tile's time and its spoken label "Clip, {time}, {duration}" use `wallClockTimeText`, which formats a `ClipLocalTime.wallClock`'s fields with the same two patterns and no conversion (`timeOfDayText` converts to local time first; both call one private `_timeOfDay`). `now` is `clockProvider.now()` at build time: the text is not recomputed at midnight until the next rebuild.

**Remove on a missing tile in To tag and Skipped** (final fix wave, D-116; `tagging/views/clip_tile_row.dart`, `remove_missing_clip.dart`):
1. A picked clip whose address cannot be read is only flagged `missing` and stays in To tag or Skipped (D-116), so its tile is `TtMissingClipTile` (the reel page's component, copy and tokens) and `ClipTileRow` gives it **Remove** (`onRemoveMissing`, `clipRemove`; at large text it is the icon alone, still named "Remove"; 48 dp target). A row with such a tile is as tall as `TtMissingClipTile.heightFor` needs at the phone's text scale (the same rule as the reel page, D-87: nothing grows at 1x, the picture tiles keep 3:4 at the top of the row). The tile still takes no tap or long press and cannot be chosen, tagged, skipped or restored.
2. Tap → `RemoveMissingClip.removeMissingClip(id, controller.removeMissing)` (a mixin of both screens' state): a clip already being removed is ignored (a double tap writes once); the controller's method (`ToTagController.removeMissing` / `SkippedController.removeMissing`, both `ClipsRepository.removeMissingFromInbox`, "Tagging data changes" above) and the messenger are looked up before the write, so the snack and Undo work after the screen has gone.
3. A record that is not empty → "Missing clip removed" with **Undo** on the root messenger (`ReelSnacks.showUndo`, the reel page's copy). Empty (the clip was found again, or is gone) → nothing is shown. A throw is logged by type (`Removing a missing clip failed: StateError`) and shows "Something went wrong. Try again."; the tile stays.
4. The list updates by itself (the database stream): the clip's row is gone, and the clip too when it is in no reel and no list. Undo (`undoAll` on `InboxRemoveUndo`) puts the row back in its state and the clip back with its id, still flagged missing. Applies to picked and MediaStore missing clips alike.
**In tests:** `clips_repository_inbox_test.dart` (`removeMissingFromInbox` and its Undo), `skipped_controller_test.dart`, `to_tag_controller_test.dart`, `skipped_screen_test.dart` and `to_tag_actions_test.dart` (group "Remove on a missing clip", on the real database), goldens `to_tag_missing_{1x,2x}` and `skipped_missing_{1x,2x}` (macOS only).

**The access banner** (`media_access/views/media_access_banner.dart › MediaAccessBanner`, used by both screens): watches `mediaAccessControllerProvider`; `value?.banner` null (loading or error) or `none` → nothing; `manual` → `TtBanner(info)`; `lost` → `TtBanner(warning)`; the text button calls `MediaAccessController.allow()` (D-75).

**The Shooting mode card** (`shooting_mode/views/shooting_mode_section.dart › ShootingModeSection`, in `ReelsScreen`):
1. Column: `MediaAccessBanner`, then the card with the screen gutter, 4 dp above and 8 dp below.
2. While the stored Shooting mode has not been read, no card. If reading it fails, the "off" card.
3. The whole card is a labelled group (`semanticLabel` = `shootingModeCardSemantics('on' | 'off')`, "Shooting mode, on" or "..., off"); its switch, camera button and rows keep their own nodes inside it. The switch (`TtSwitch`, label "Shooting mode") calls `ShootingModeController.setOn(value)`; a scan that is running when it is turned off is handled by the controller (D-75), the card shows what is stored.
4. On: title "Shooting mode on"; subtitle `shootingOnSubtitle(time)`, or `shootingOnSubtitleManual(time)` when the access view is known and not `full`; **Open camera** → `openCamera()`; backdrop `clipThumbnail(latestPending(pending) ?? firstShowableClip, width, cache)` or none (`firstShowableClipProvider` is `ClipsRepository.watchFirstShowableClip`: the lowest-id MediaStore clip that is not missing, the prototype's "first clip that is not missing").
5. `rows` (0 to 2 `TtCardRow`s of `kind: TtCardRowKind.link`, D-79, D-81; References will be first): the "Add earlier videos" row (history icon, subtitle "Shot before Shooting mode was on", chevron, label "Add earlier videos, shot before Shooting mode was on"), whenever access is `full` and the Shooting state was read (`shootingState.hasValue`); it calls `showAddEarlierVideos(context)` and stays after an add (D-90). The **off** card gets the same row in its `rows` (subtitle "From your camera folder", label "Add earlier videos, from your camera folder"), drawn in the off card's hairline look; it is hidden in Manual mode, while access or the Shooting state is loading, and when the Shooting state could not be read. `toTagStrip`: `TtCardRow` (`kind: TtCardRowKind.strip`) "N clips to tag" / "Tag now" when any clip waits; it calls `context.go(Routes.toTag)`.
6. Below the card: while Shooting mode is off (the stored state is known) and at least one clip waits, `TtBanner(attention, filled sell icon, chevron)` titled `clipsWaitingTitle(count)`, with the body `clipsWaitingBody(waitingDurationText(l10n, sum of the waiting clips' durationMs))`; tapping it calls `context.go(Routes.toTag)`. `waitingDurationText` is the prototype's `fmt.mins`: `seconds < 60` → `durationSeconds(round(seconds))`; else `minutes = round(seconds / 60)`, `< 60` → `durationMinutes(minutes)`, else `durationHoursMinutes(minutes ~/ 60, minutes % 60)` (so 59.5 s reads "60 s" and 59.5 min "1 h 0 min"). The order in the column is the prototype's: access banner, card, this banner. With Shooting mode on the card shows its own strip and this banner is not drawn.
7. The waiting clips come from `tagging/view_models/pending_clips.dart › pendingClipsProvider` (`watchInbox(pending)`, no scan, no verify).

**Add earlier videos** (`tagging/views/add_earlier_videos.dart › showAddEarlierVideos(context)`, D-78, D-79):
1. Opens the sheet with `showTtSheet`; reads the `ProviderContainer` and the localizations first (the sheet may be gone before the add ends).
2. The sheet watches `earlierVideosControllerProvider` (auto-dispose, Today's count read when it opens). Two tall `TtListRow`s, **Today** (the date, `MMMEd`, under it; its count at the end) and **Choose dates…** ("Up to 10 years back"; a chevron). **Every row is off** (`TtListRow.off`: AA text, a disabled button, no opacity) while `loading` (the count has no value yet), `_finding` (the chosen dates are counted) or an add runs (`_adding`). Today is off too when its count is 0 or could not be read.
   - Count loading → Today's count is a `TtSkeletonCount` (48 × 16 dp); Today is announced "Today, checking for new videos".
   - Count ready → `TtRowCount`: "N new" in a pill, or plain "none" for 0 (`earlierRowCount`). Announced "Today, {n} new video(s). Adds them to To tag" or "Today, no new videos"; Choose dates… "Choose dates, up to 10 years back".
   - Count failed (Today's, or the count of chosen dates, below) → a `TtBanner(warning)` "Couldn't check your videos." with **Try again** (`ref.invalidate`, or counting the same dates again; a tap while a count runs does nothing: `_find` and the button check `_finding`) under the subtitle, and **the rows stay**: Today has no count and is announced "Today, not checked" (off), or, after a failed count of dates, its count; Choose dates… stays usable.
3. **Today** tap: `_add(const EarlierToday())`, which returns at once if an add runs, sets `_adding = today` (Today shows "Adding…" and a spinner), and calls `EarlierVideosController.add`.
   - ids returned, not empty → the outcome `_Added(ids)` goes to `showAddEarlierVideos`' `announce`, then the sheet pops.
   - `[]` → no outcome; `_adding` is cleared and the sheet stays (the controller counted again, so the row shows the truth).
   - it throws → outcome `_AddFailed`, the sheet pops.
4. **Choose dates…** tap (`_chooseDates`, ignored while `_choosing` or an add runs): the row takes the focus (`_chooseFocus`), then `showTtDateRangePicker(firstDate: ten years before today, lastDate: today, currentDate: today, initialDateRange: _lastRange, labels)`, with today from `clockProvider`. `_lastRange` is null in a new sheet, and is set by `_find` to the dates of every Find videos, so the picker **opens on the last dates chosen in this sheet**. Close or back → nothing (and `_lastRange` is unchanged); the focus goes back to the row. Days → `_find(EarlierCustom(start, end))` (one day: `end == start`, also when only the first day was chosen).
   - The picker (`taketray_ui`): Close, "Choose dates" (the title wraps at large text and the bar grows: `TtAppBar.wrapTitle`), Clear (only while days are chosen); the summary (a polite live region): "Tap the first day / Then tap the last day.", "{day} – … / Tap the last day, or Find videos for {day} only.", "{from} – {to} / {n} days", or "{day} / 1 day"; the quick ranges Yesterday, Last week (the whole week before this one, from the locale's first day), This month, Last month, which only fill the calendar (nothing is counted); the calendar; "Find videos", off until a day is chosen. The tap rules: first tap the first day, second tap the last (the same day: one day), before the first day moves it, after a full range starts again. Future days and days before the first date are off; ‹ › stop at their months; the month name opens the year and month grid.
5. `_find`: `_finding` shows "{dates} · Checking…" under Choose dates… with a `TtRowSpinner` in place of the chevron (announced "Choose dates. {dates} · Checking…"; the spinner "Checking your videos from {dates}") and turns both rows off; `EarlierVideosController.countIn(range)`. `{dates}` is `ttDayText` of the first and last day ("8 Oct – 10 Oct"): the phone's `MMMd`, with the year (`yMMMd`) when a date is not in this year, and no-break spaces.
   - it throws → the count-failure state of point 2 (Try again counts the same dates again).
   - the sheet is gone meanwhile → nothing more happens.
   - count 0 → a `TtDialog` "No new videos", "No new videos between {from} and {to}." (the same date text; one day: "No new videos on {day}."), one **OK**; the sheet stays open.
   - count N ≥ 1 → a `TtDialog` "{count, plural, one{1 video found} other{{count} videos found}}", "{count, plural, one{Add it to To tag?} other{Add them to To tag?}}", **Cancel** and **Add**. Cancel or a tap outside: nothing is added, the sheet stays open. Add: `_add(range)` as in point 3 (`_adding = dates`: "{dates} · Adding…" and a spinner), so the snack's count is what was really inserted. Whenever the Choose dates… row is usable again without the sheet closing (Cancel, OK, a failed count, or an add that found nothing) `_restoreFocus` gives it the focus back after the next frame: the row has no ink well, so no focus, while it is off.
6. `announce` (does nothing if the opening screen is gone): `_Added` → `container.listen(earlierVideosControllerProvider)` keeps the controller alive; `showTtSnack("{count} clip(s) added to To tag", check icon, Undo)` with `count = ids.length`; when the snack closes the listener is closed. `_AddFailed` → `showTtSnack("Couldn't add videos. Try again.")`.
7. Undo: `EarlierVideosController.undo(ids)` (D-75: only clips still waiting and in no reel go). A throw is logged by type and shown as the snack "Couldn't undo. Try again.". `showTtSnack` ignores a second tap on Undo and, after the snack closes, there is no button.
8. An add changes nothing about the card's link: it stays, on and off (D-90; before, the first add of a session hid it).

**In tests:** `test/support/pump_screen.dart › pumpScreen` (theme, localizations, a router with a `/to-tag` marker route, a text scale, "remove animations"), `test/support/view_model_fakes.dart` (`FakeAccess`, `FakeShooting`, `FakeToTag`), `test/support/clips.dart › testClip`, `test/support/accessibility.dart` (`expectTtAccessible`), `test/support/test_app.dart › testApp` (whole-app tests), and `test/flutter_test_config.dart` (loads the app fonts, so layouts measure real text). The sheet and the full flows use `VmHarness` (real database and queue, `FakeMediaBridge`).

### Home, the Reels tab (`lib/features/reels/`, `lib/core/midnight_tick.dart`, D-86)

**Opening Home** (`views/reels_screen.dart › ReelsScreen`, the `/reels` route):
1. The shell builds the Reels branch once and keeps it (`StatefulShellRoute.indexedStack`), so its scroll position and its filter survive a visit to another tab. `build` watches `homeControllerProvider` and, through `ShootingModeSection`, the Shooting mode state, the access state, the waiting clips and `localDayProvider`.
2. `HomeController.build` (`view_models/home_controller.dart`) awaits `reelSummariesProvider` (`ReelsRepository.watchReelSummaries`: one statement, every reel with its clip count, missing count, live length and cover clip, newest first, D-84) and reads `thumbnailCacheProvider`'s value (null until ready). It returns `HomeState`: `applyFilter(all, filter)` for `reels`, `filterCounts(all)` for `counts`, and for each shown reel its `clipThumbnail(cover, homeCoverSize, cache)` (null when the reel has no cover clip). Any change to a reel, a link or a clip emits a new list and rebuilds the state; the filter field of the notifier is kept.
3. While `home` is null (the first list is not read yet, or the stream failed) the screen shows the app bar and the Shooting mode section and nothing else: no empty state, no chips, no FAB.
4. Body, top to bottom (a `CustomScrollView`): `ShootingModeSection` (the access banner, the card, the clips-waiting banner); when `home.hasReels`, `_FilterChips` (`TtPillTabs<ReelFilter>`); then one of: no reel at all → `TtEmptyState(noReels)` "Plan your first reel" with **New reel**; reels but none passing the filter → `TtEmptyState(emptyReel)` "No reels marked {status}" with that status's line and a tonal **Show all reels**; else a `SliverGrid.builder` of `_ReelCard`s (two columns); then room for the nav pill and the FAB. A `PositionedDirectional` `TtFab` "New reel" sits at `TtNavPill.fabBottom(context)` while `hasReels`.
5. **A chip** calls `HomeController.setFilter(f)`: the notifier stores `f` and, if it has a state, composes a new one from the last list at once (no read). **Show all reels** is `setFilter(ReelFilter.all)`.
6. **A card** is `TtReelCard(name, status: statusKindOf(status), statusLabel: statusName(l10n, status), meta, missingText, emptyLabel: "No clips yet", cover, semanticLabel)`. `meta` is `reelCardClips(n)` for a reel with no clips ("0 clips") and else `reelCardMeta(clips, formatClipDuration(liveDurationMs))` ("3 clips · 1:05"; the length leaves out missing clips). `missingText` is "{n} missing" when `missingCount > 0`. The label is `reelCardSemantics(name, status, n)` or `reelCardSemanticsMissing(...)`. Tapping calls `context.push(Routes.reel(id))`.
7. **New reel** (the FAB and the empty-state button) calls `showReelForm(context)`; see "Creating and editing a reel". When the sheet saves, the list stream emits and the card appears behind the closing sheet.
8. **The reel location** `/reels/:id` is a child of `/reels`. Its `redirect` accepts a positive whole number and sends anything else to `/reels` (logging "Unknown location; showing Reels", never the location). A good id builds `ReelDetailScreen` (see "Opening a reel"). System back pops it and Home is as it was.

**The midnight tick** (`core/midnight_tick.dart › localDayProvider`):
1. `ShootingModeSection.build` and `ToTagScreen.build` call `ref.watch(localDayProvider)` (the value is not used). The first watch starts the provider: `tick()` reads `clockProvider.now().toLocal()`, emits `DateTime(y, m, d)` (local midnight of today) and sets `Timer(DateTime(y, m, d + 1) - now, tick)`.
2. When the timer fires, `tick()` reads the clock again. A new day is emitted (the watching widgets rebuild and read the clock: `shootingTimeText` sees "yesterday" and adds the date) and the next timer is set for the next midnight. The same day (the timer fired early, or the clock was moved back) emits nothing and sets the timer again.
3. When no widget watches it any more the provider is disposed and `ref.onDispose` cancels the timer and closes the stream.
4. On a day with a clock change the delay is 23 or 25 hours, because it is the difference of two local `DateTime`s.
5. **Coming back to the app** (`AppLifecycleState.resumed`): a `WidgetsBindingObserver` added with the provider (and removed in `ref.onDispose`) calls `tick()` again. A phone that slept through midnight may not have run its timer yet, so the new day is emitted at once; the same day emits nothing. `tick()` cancels the pending timer before setting the next one, so a resume never leaves two. The other states (`inactive`, `hidden`, `paused`) do nothing.

**When the camera cannot open** (`shooting_mode/views/open_camera.dart › openCameraOrSay`): the **Open camera** buttons (the Shooting mode card, To tag's strip, To tag's "All caught up") call it. It looks up the root messenger and the words, awaits `ShootingModeController.openCamera()` and, on `false`, shows "Couldn't open the camera." (`showTtSnackOn`). A camera that opens shows nothing. The FAB rises over the snack by itself (D-82).

### Creating and editing a reel (`lib/features/reels/`, D-85)

1. A screen calls `views/reel_form_sheet.dart › showReelForm(context, {reel})` (Home's **New reel**, the reel page's ⋮ ▸ Reel details; both arrive in Tasks 6 and 8). It first runs `views/undo_snacks.dart › ReelSnacks.of(context)`, which looks up the root messenger (step 7), the `ProviderContainer` and the words, then `showTtSheet` opens `_ReelFormSheet` on the root navigator.
2. `_ReelFormSheetState.build` watches `reelFormControllerProvider(reel?.id)` (`view_models/reel_form_controller.dart`; null `reelId` is "new") and draws a `TtSheet` titled "New reel" or "Reel details": the name `TtTextField` (autofocus, `maxLength` 40, counter `n/40`), the Shot ideas field (multiline, `maxLength` 1,000, counter text empty below 900 characters), and, when editing, the status radios (`_StatusRadioRow`, in a `Semantics(role: radioGroup)` named "Status"). Typing calls `clearErrors()`.
3. **Create reel** / **Save** (and Enter in the name field) call `_save`. It ignores the tap while `state.saving`, then awaits `ReelFormController.save(name:, description:, status:)` (the status only when editing).
4. `ReelFormController.save`: when a save is running it returns that save's future (`_inFlight`, single-flight). Otherwise `_save` sets `saving`, reads `ReelsRepository.watchAll().first`, and checks the name (`checkReelName`, then a clash with another reel's `reelNameKey`; when editing, the reel's own name in any case is never a clash, as in `rename`, so a reel that shares its name with a restored or imported one can still save) and the description (`checkReelDescription`) together. Any problem goes into the state (`nameError`, `descriptionError`, `clashingName` = the other reel's stored name) and `save` returns null.
5. Otherwise a new reel is `ReelsRepository.create(name, description:)`. An edit is `_edit`: `getById`, then `rename`, `setDescription` and `setStatus`, each only when its value changed, the name first; it returns the stored reel. An `InvalidInputException` from the repository (another reel took the name after step 4 looked) goes back into the state the same way. Any other error is logged by type, `saving` is cleared and it is rethrown.
6. `_save` on a reel: `Navigator.pop` (when still mounted) then `onSaved(reel)`, which shows "{name} created" (`reelCreatedSnack`) or "Saved" (`reelSavedSnack`) through `ReelSnacks.show`. On null the sheet stays open, with the message under the field (`reelNameEmpty` / `reelNameTooLong` / `reelNameDuplicate(clashingName)`). On a thrown error the sheet sets `_failed`, and its footer (`views/sheet_failure.dart › SheetFooter`) shows a warning `TtBanner` "Something went wrong. Try again." (`reelActionFailed`) above the buttons, in the sheet, because a snack would be behind the modal sheet. The sheet stays open with the text kept. `_failed` is cleared at the next Save, at typing and when a status radio is chosen.
7. `ReelSnacks.show` / `showUndo` call `showTtSnackOn(messenger, ...)` (`packages/taketray_ui/lib/src/components/snack.dart`) on the **root messenger**: `features/shell/views/root_messenger.dart › rootMessengerKeyProvider` is given to `MaterialApp.router(scaffoldMessengerKey:)` in `app.dart`, and `rootMessengerOf(context)` reads `key.currentState`. A snack there outlives the sheet and every page that pops.

**Changing a reel's status** (`views/reel_status_menu.dart`; the reel page wires it in Task 8):
1. The reel header's status pill opens a `TtMenuButton` whose items are `reelStatusItems(l10n, reel.status)`: one `TtMenuItem<ReelStatus>` per status with the filled status icon, the name, the description as `subtitle` and `checked` for the current one (announced as a radio menu item).
2. Choosing one calls `changeReelStatus(context, reel, status)`. The same status as now returns at once. Otherwise it captures `ReelSnacks.of(context)`, then `ReelActions.setStatus(id, status)` (`view_models/reel_actions.dart`, `ReelsRepository.setStatus`), then shows "Marked as {status}" (`reelStatusChangedSnack`) with no Undo. A failure is logged by type and shows `reelActionFailed`.

### The delete reel dialog and its Undo (`lib/features/reels/views/delete_reel_dialog.dart`, D-85, D-128)

1. A screen calls `showDeleteReelDialog(context, reel)` (the reel page's ⋮ ▸ Delete reel, Task 8). Before awaiting anything it captures the words, `ReelSnacks.of(context)`, `GoRouter.of(context)`, the container and `ReelActions`. Then it reads the reel's editing session once (`EditSessionsRepository.editState`; a read that throws is logged by type and the dialog shows without the copies line).
2. `showTtDialog` shows a `TtDialog` with the delete icon, "Delete {name}?" and, as its content, `_DeleteBody`: a `TtDialogNote` ("Your videos stay in your gallery. Only this reel and its tags are removed."), then **only when the session's folder holds copies** the copies line (`_copiesLine`, R8): VN "The {n} VN copies ({size}) will also be deleted, and the VN project can't be edited after that." (`deleteReelVnCopies`), CapCut or Edits "The {n} copies in Movies/TakeTray/{folder} ({size}) will also be deleted." (`deleteReelFolderCopies`; the session's folder, `byteSizeText` of the copies' bytes). Buttons **Cancel** / **Delete reel** (danger).
3. **The dialog decides its own pops.** `_DeleteBody` holds a `PopScope(canPop: false)`: every button (`TtDialog` calls `maybePop(value)`), Android back and a tap outside arrive in `_onPop(didPop, result)`. While the delete runs (`_busy`) they are ignored. Otherwise `true` (Delete reel) starts the delete; anything else pops the dialog with `false` and nothing happens.
4. `ReelActions.confirmDelete(id)` is single-flight per reel id: a second call while one runs returns the first call's future. It first calls `EditSessionService.beforeReelDelete(id)` (D-123: cancels the reel's copy job, deletes every copy through the guard, ends the editing session; a throw, such as `CopiesNotDeleted`, deletes nothing), then `ReelsRepository.delete(id)` (one transaction: the reel, its links, references and any editing rows go; the orphan sweep reads then deletes the clips left in no reel and no list) and returns the `DeleteReelUndo` record. A failure is logged by type and rethrown.
5. In the dialog: success → the record is kept on `_DeleteRun` and the dialog pops `true`. `CopiesNotDeleted` → the dialog **stays**, nothing was deleted, and "Couldn't delete the copies. Try again." (`copiesNotDeleted`, a live region in the error colour) shows under the text; **Delete reel** tries again. Any other failure → the dialog pops `false` with `_DeleteRun.failed` set.
6. Back in the dialog function: a record that is not empty (`reel != null`) goes to `ReelSnacks.showUndo("{name} deleted", record)`, an **Undo** snack on the root messenger; then `router.go(Routes.reels)`. A failure shows `reelActionFailed` and leaves the app where it is.
7. **Undo** (`ReelSnacks._undo`): `ReelsRepository.undoAll([record])` (`data/repositories/undo_writer.dart`, see "Undo") puts the reel, its links and the swept clips back with the same ids, **without its editing session** (R8). The snack runs the action once however often it is tapped (`showTtSnackOn` guards it). If it throws, the type is logged and "Couldn't undo. Try again." (`undoFailed`) is shown, or, for `CopiesNotDeleted` (an undo that would take clips out of a reel being edited, whose copies could not be deleted first; also the tag player's own undo), "Couldn't delete the copies. Try again." (`copiesNotDeleted`). Because the messenger, container and words were captured in step 1, Undo works after the dialog's screen has gone, until the snack closes. The remove, move and add snacks of Tasks 8 to 10 use the same `ReelSnacks` (`showUndo`, `showUndoAll`).

### Opening a reel (`lib/features/reels/`, `lib/router/`, D-87)

**Getting there:** a card on Home does `context.push(Routes.reel(id))` through `OpenOnce.open` (`views/open_once.dart`): a tap in the window after the first (`kThemeAnimationDuration * 2`) does nothing, so a double tap opens one page; the window is not the push's future, which a `go` (the delete) never completes (`/reels/:id`, a child of the Reels branch, so the nav pill stays and the Reels tab stays selected). `app_router.dart` accepts only a positive whole number for the id; anything else goes to `/reels`. A tile on the page does `context.push(Routes.clip(reelId, clipId))` through `OpenOnce` in the same way (`/reels/:id/clip/:clipId`, the clip viewer, see "Watching a clip"), with the same rule for the clip id.

**The Done editing location:** `Routes.reelDoneEditing(id)` (`/reels/<id>?done=1`, the copies reminder's tap, D-124) builds `ReelDetailScreen(reelId:, askDoneEditing: true)`; any other value of `done` is `false`.

**Building the page** (`views/reel_detail_screen.dart › ReelDetailScreen`, `view_models/reel_detail_controller.dart`, `view_models/reel_edit_state.dart`):
1. `build` watches `reelDetailControllerProvider(reelId)`, `thumbnailCacheProvider`, `localDayProvider` (so a day's year follows the calendar), the phone's 24-hour setting, `reelEditStateProvider(reelId)` (the editing session and the split, D-128) and `copyJobControllerProvider` (through `runningCopyJobOf(job, reelId)`: the reel's copy job while it runs, else null).
2. `ReelDetailController.build(reelId)` starts three watches: `reelByIdProvider` (`ReelsRepository.watchById`), `reelClipsProvider` (`ClipsRepository.watchReelClips`: the clips oldest first, each with its reel count and `addedAt`) and `reelClipTimesProvider` (`ClipTimeService.localTimes` over those clips, D-83). It awaits the first two and **reads the third without awaiting it** (`.value ?? {}`).
3. The reel is `null` (no such reel, or it was deleted) → the state is `null`. Else, once per open, **it checks the copies**: `unawaited(guarded(... EditSessionService.verifyCopies(reelId)))` (D-128; a reel with no session or no copies returns at once without asking the phone; the bridge's `existingCopies` says which copy files exist, and the rows of the others are removed, so their clips become new again; an error is logged by type and nothing changes). And the first time it has clips, **it starts the verify**: `unawaited(guarded(... MediaVerifier.verify(clips)))`, once per open (a notifier field), never for a reel with no clips. Without full access the verifier skips every clip and writes nothing; an error is logged and nothing is flagged (D-72).
4. It returns `ReelDetailState`: `days` from `groupByShootingDay<ReelClip>(clips, (c) => c.clip, times)` (the date of each clip's `wallClock`, or Dart's local date for a clip with no time yet; day numbers count calendar days), `clipCount`, `missingCount`, `liveDurationMs` (the clips that are not missing) and `times`.
5. The verify flags a gone clip → the list stream emits → the controller is built again (no second verify). The times arrive (or a new list needs new ones) → built again with `times`, so the days are regrouped and the tiles switch from the phone's time to the time where the clip was shot. Both are quiet redraws: the old state stays on screen until the new one is ready.
6. **The editing split** (`reelEditStateProvider(reelId)`, D-128): it awaits `reelEditSessionProvider(reelId)` (`EditSessionsRepository.watchEditState`) and `reelClipsProvider`, reads the times without awaiting, and is `null` without a session. Else `splitForEditing(clips, sentIds:, liveCopyIds: {recorded copies' clip ids})` (D-119): "In your edit" is sent ∪ live copies, "New since you started editing" the rest. `groupByShootingDay` runs over all the reel's clips and each part keeps the days that hold its clips, with the reel's Day numbers. The state also has the session (editor, folder, start), the copies' count and bytes, both counts (missing clips included) and `newLiveCount` (how many new clips are on the phone). It follows the session, the clips and the times.
