7. **`askDoneEditing`:** in `build`, once the edit state has a value and the page has not decided yet (`_doneAsked`, per page), the page decides once: a session and no running copy job of the reel → `showDoneEditingDialog(context, ref, reelId)` after the frame; else nothing. A later session, a redraw, the viewer and back, or another tab and back never ask again.

**What the screen shows,** top to bottom:
1. The app bar: **Back** (`pop`, or `go(/reels)` when nothing can pop) and, once the reel is known, **Add videos** (`library_add`, `openAddVideos(AddToReel(id))` through an `OpenOnce`, "The folder browser") and the ⋮ menu. It has no title.
1a. At the top of the list, `MediaAccessBanner` (nothing with full access; "Manual mode" or "TakeTray can't see your videos" otherwise, D-115), so the page explains why Add videos sent the user to the photo picker, or took them back here when access was lost.
2. While there is no state: a skeleton day header (read as "Loading") and 9 skeleton tiles. A first read that fails stays here (no error state is designed).
3. `TtReelHeader<ReelStatus>` (name wrapped fully; the status pill opens the status menu, `reelStatusItems` and `changeReelStatus`; "{n} clips · {m:ss}" or "No clips yet"; "{m} missing"; Shot ideas with Show more / Show less).
3a. **While the reel is being edited, or its copy job runs:** `ReelEditBanner` (`views/reel_edit_banner.dart`, a `TtBanner(kind: vn)`, actions under the text). A running job of the reel: "Copying clips for {editor}" / "{done} of {total} clips" and **Show** (`push(Routes.copyProgressOf(jobId))` through an `OpenOnce`); no Done editing. Else VN: "Editing in VN · {size}" / "{n} copies in Movies/TakeTray/{folder}", **Done editing** and **Add more clips**; CapCut or Edits: "Editing in {editor}" (+ " · {size}" with copies) / "Sent on {date}" (+ " · {n} copies in Movies/TakeTray/{folder}"), **Done editing** only. **Done editing** → `showDoneEditingDialog` (see "Done editing"); **Add more clips** → `push(Routes.editSelectOf(reelId, add: true))` through the page's `OpenOnce` for the picking screen.
4. No clips: `TtEmptyState(emptyReel)` "No clips yet", "Tag clips from To tag, or add videos from your phone.", **Go to To tag** (`context.go(Routes.toTag)`) and a text **Add videos** (as the app bar's).
5. Else, with a session, `reelEditSlivers` (`views/reel_edit_sections.dart`): when there are new clips a `ReelEditSectionHeader` "New since you started editing" / "{n} clips · not in your edit yet" (with **Copy to VN folder** for VN, `tonal`, small: `push(Routes.editSelectOf(reelId, add: true))`, off while the reel's copy job runs or when every new clip is missing; no button for CapCut or Edits until Task 12) and that part's days; when the editor has any, "In your edit" / "{n} clips · in the VN folder" or "{n} clips · sent to {editor} on {date}" and that part's days. The header is `TtSceneHeader`'s look with wrapping text and the button in a `Wrap` (at the end while it fits, under the text when not). Without a session (or while it is not known yet) `reelDaySlivers` over all the days. In both, for each `ShootingDay`: `TtDayHeader("Day {n} · {ttDayWeekdayText(date)}", "{k} clips")`, then a lazy three-column `SliverGrid` inside a `SliverLayoutBuilder` (the real width; `_DayGrid`, built from a `ReelTiles` the page fills: times, cache, locale, 24-hour, the choice, and what a tap, a long press and **Remove** do). The date is `MMMEd` in the phone's locale with the weekday ("Wed, Oct 28"), and `yMMMEd` with the year when it is not this year ("Tue, Dec 30, 2025"), no-break spaces. A clip → `TtClipTile` (time, length, reel count, horizontal, label "Clip, {time}, {duration}[, in {k} reels]"; tap → viewer; long press → starts choosing, see "Managing clips in a reel"). A clip flagged missing → `TtMissingClipTile` with **Remove**.
6. **Rows that hold a missing tile:** the day's grid uses `mainAxisExtent = max(3:4 height, TtMissingClipTile.heightFor(...))`, so the tile is at its real size at the phone's text scale; its neighbours are `Align`ed to the top of their cell at 3:4. A day with no missing tile uses the 3:4 grid.
7. Room under the grid for the floating nav pill.

**Edit video (Phase 8, D-126):** `TtFab("Edit video", movie_edit)` above the nav pill (`TtNavPill.fabBottom`), shown when `state.clipCount > state.missingCount` (a live clip), `!selection.selecting`, and `reelEditStateProvider(reelId)` has a value that is `null` (the reel has no editing session: R1; until the database has answered the button waits). It opens `Routes.editSelectOf(reelId)` through its own `OpenOnce`. The list gets `FAB height + 16 dp` more room at its end while the button is shown. See "Picking clips and the editor sheet".

**The ⋮ menu:** **Reel details** → `showReelForm(context, reel:)`; **Select clips** → `ReelSelection.begin()` ("Managing clips in a reel"); **Delete reel** → `showDeleteReelDialog` ("The delete dialog and its Undo": it deletes, shows "{name} deleted" with Undo and goes to `/reels`).

**Remove on a missing tile:**
1. The screen looks up `ReelSnacks.of(context)`, the words and the notifier **before** awaiting.
2. `ReelDetailController.removeMissing(clipId)`: a second call for the same clip while one runs returns the same future. It reads the reel's clips; if the clip is not in the reel, or is no longer flagged missing (a verify found the video again), it returns an empty `RemoveUndo` and changes nothing. Else `EditSessionService.beforeClipsLeaveReel(reelId, {clipId})` (its copy is deleted first during an editing session, D-123; `CopiesNotDeleted` → nothing removed, the error goes to the view), then `ClipsRepository.removeClipsFromReel(reelId, [clipId])` (one transaction; the orphan sweep may delete the clip row, and the record keeps it, D-84).
3. `removeMissing` returns `({record, copiesDeleted})`. A record that is not empty → "Missing clip removed" (or, when `copiesDeleted` is more than 0, "Missing clip removed. Its copy in the folder was deleted.") with **Undo** on the root messenger. Undo (`ReelsRepository.undo`) puts back the link with its `addedAt` and, if it was swept, the same clip with its id; it still works after the page has been left. An empty record shows nothing. `CopiesNotDeleted` shows "Couldn't delete the copies. Try again." and any other thrown error "Something went wrong. Try again."; the tile stays.

**Removing or moving clips while editing (R8, D-128):** `ReelClipActions.removeFromReel` / `moveToReel` delete the clips' copies first and return `copiesDeleted`. More than 0 → the remove snack becomes "{n} clips removed from {reel}. … Its copy in the folder was deleted." (`removedWithCopies` + `copiesDeletedSuffix`: "Its copy…" for one copy, "Their copies…" for more) and the move snack "Moved to {reel}. Its copy in the folder was deleted." (`movedToCopiesSnack`, in `pickAndPerform`). `CopiesNotDeleted` removes or moves nothing: the remove says "Couldn't delete the copies. Try again." in a snack and keeps the choice; the move says it in the picker sheet (`_copiesFailed`, `SheetFooter`, D-85), or in a snack when the sheet was dragged away. The clip viewer's **Remove** does the same for its one clip (the snack "Removed from {reel}. The video is still in your gallery. Its copy in the folder was deleted."; `CopiesNotDeleted` → the same snack text as above, the clip stays and the viewer stays on it). Undo of a remove brings a VN clip back under "New since…" (its copy is gone) and a CapCut or Edits clip under "In your edit" (its sent row stays).

**Done editing** (`lib/features/editors/views/done_editing_dialog.dart › showDoneEditingDialog(context, ref, reelId, {suggestPublished = true})`, D-128):
1. A set of reel ids per `ProviderContainer` (an `Expando`) makes it one question per reel at a time; a second call returns at once. It captures the words, `ReelSnacks`, the root navigator and the container, then reads `editState` and the reel once (a read that throws is logged by type and nothing is shown). No session, no reel, or a running copy job of the reel (the job controller's, or, while it follows none, `CopyBridge.current()`) → nothing.
2. With copies: `TtDialog` "Have you exported your video?" with `_CopiesBody` (VN `doneEditingVnBody` with the reel's name in bold through `boldMark`/`boldSpans`; CapCut/Edits `doneEditingCopiesBody` with "Instagram Edits" in full; "This frees {size}" from the recorded copies) and `TtDialogNote("Your original clips are safe in your gallery.")`, **Not yet** / **Delete copies** (danger). Without copies: "Done editing {name}?", "TakeTray stops showing which clips are in your {editor} project. Nothing is deleted.", **Not yet** / **Done editing**.
3. On yes: `EditSessionService.doneEditing(reelId)`. Any throw → logged by type, "Couldn't delete the copies. Try again." (or "Something went wrong. Try again." without copies), nothing else. Success → "Copies deleted · {freed} freed" (plus "{n} files were changed outside TakeTray, so they were left in the folder." when `leftBehind > 0`) or "Done editing {name}"; the set entry is released.
4. Unless `suggestPublished` is false: `Future.delayed(publishedOfferDelay)` (900 ms), then `showMarkStatusDialogIn(navigator.overlay.context, container, reelId, published)` when the navigator is still mounted (it does nothing for a reel that is gone or Published); a throw is logged by type.

**A reel that is gone** (deleted here, deleted elsewhere, or an id that names none): `detail.hasValue && detail.value == null` → after the frame, once, `context.go(Routes.reels)`.

**In tests:** `test/support/pump_screen.dart › pumpScreen` (with a `/reels` marker, a `/reels/:id` marker and a `/reels/:id/clip/:clipId` marker), `test/support/reel_videos.dart` (`reelHarness`, `reelVideo`, `putInReel`: videos on the fake phone and in a reel, or in a reel and gone from the phone), `testApp`, and `h.bridge.gate('zoneOffsetsMs')` to hold the times back.

### Managing clips in a reel (`lib/features/reels/`, D-88)

**Choosing clips** (`view_models/reel_selection.dart › ReelSelection(reelId)`, `views/reel_detail_screen.dart`):
1. A long press on a tile calls `start(clipId)`; **Select clips** in the ⋮ menu calls `begin()` (choosing with 0 chosen). Both only work for a clip that is in the reel's list and not missing (the list is `reelClipsProvider(reelId)`); before that list is read nothing can be chosen.
2. While `state.selecting`: the page shows `TtAppBar(selection: true, liveRegion: true)` (close, "{n} selected", which a screen reader reads each time the count changes, Add to another reel, Move to another reel, Remove from this reel, ⋮ → **Edit video** (hidden while the reel is in an editing session), **Select all**, **Share**; Phase 8, D-126) and every `TtClipTile` has `selected` set. A tap or long press calls `toggle(clipId)` and never opens the viewer. `toggle` on the last chosen clip clears the selection (`selecting` false). A missing tile is not selectable; its **Remove** works as before. **Select all** calls `selectAll()`: every clip that is not missing. **Edit video** reads the chosen clips in shooting order (`inShootingOrder`), ends choosing (`clear()`) and opens `Routes.editSelectOf(reelId, clipIds: …)`; **Share** calls `openShare(context, ref, chosenInOrder)` (nothing for an empty choice; the choice stays; a second tap while one share opens does nothing). See "Picking clips and the editor sheet".
3. The selection follows the list: when `reelClipsProvider` emits, chosen clips that are now missing or no longer in the reel are dropped; an emptied selection that had clips ends.
4. `PopScope(canPop: !selecting)` makes Back, the system back and **Close** call `clear()` instead of leaving the page.

**Add / Move** (`_pickReels(mode)` on the page, which hands steps 1 and 4 to 5 to `views/reel_picker_sheet.dart › pickAndPerform`, shared with the clip viewer; the sheet is `showReelPicker`):
1. The page copies the chosen ids, looks up `ReelSnacks`, `ReelClipActions` and the selection notifier **before awaiting**, and opens the picker with `clipCount`, `excludeReelId = reelId` and `perform`.
2. The sheet watches `reelSummariesProvider` (`ReelsRepository.watchReelSummaries`, D-84) and `reelByIdProvider(excludeReelId)`; `pickerSections(summaries, excludeReelId)` drops the current reel and splits **Collecting** from **Other reels**. Rows are `TtReelPickRow` (cover from `clipThumbnail`, "{n} clips"); `togglePick` keeps the picks (add: several, in tap order; move: one).
3. **New reel** opens an inline `TtTextField`. **Create** calls `ReelFormController(null).save(name:)` (the same validation as the New reel sheet: "Give this reel a name", "You already have a reel called {name}"; single-flight). A refused name shows under the field. On success `pickedAfterCreate` picks the new reel and the field closes. Another failure shows `SheetFailure` in the sheet.
4. **Confirm** (disabled with no pick and while it runs) calls `perform(pickedIds)`: add → `ReelClipActions.addToReels` (one `ClipsRepository.addClipsToReels` call for the picked reels in pick order, in one transaction: if one reel fails, nothing was added to any, and it throws); move → `moveToReel` (one `moveClips` from this reel to the picked one). On success the sheet pops with the ids. On a throw it stays open, keeps the picks and shows "Something went wrong. Try again." above its buttons until the next pick or confirm.
5. After the sheet has closed the page shows the snack on the root messenger: "Added to {A}[ and {B} | , {B} and {n} more]" or "Moved to {A}", with **Undo** (`ReelSnacks.showUndoAll`: every record undone last to first in one transaction, `ReelsRepository.undoAll`; if it fails nothing is undone and "Couldn't undo. Try again." shows). An add that changed nothing shows the plain snack with no Undo; a move that moved nothing (the chosen clips had all left the reel meanwhile) shows no snack. Then `clear()` ends the selection. `_pickReels` returns at once while a picker is open (`_picking`), so a double tap on Add or Move opens one picker. If the sheet was dragged away while the action ran, `perform` shows the snack itself when it finishes.

**Tag mode** (D-98) is the picker's third mode, opened by To tag: `excludeReelId` / `fromReelId` are null (add and move assert one), `_multi` is true, `LastTags.set` runs after the write, and its snack and Undo are in "To tag" above.

**Remove** (`_removeChosen`): `ReelClipActions.removeFromReel(reelId, ids)` → `EditSessionService.beforeClipsLeaveReel(reelId, ids)` first (D-123: during an editing session the clips' copies are deleted and the count goes into `ClipActionOutcome.copiesDeleted`; a copy not deleted throws `CopiesNotDeleted` and nothing is removed; a move calls it for the source reel the same way), then one `ClipsRepository.removeClipsFromReel` (the orphan sweep may delete a clip that was only here; the record keeps it). A non-empty record → "{n} clips removed from {reel}. The videos are still in your gallery." (singular for one clip) with **Undo**, then `clear()`. A second tap while it runs does nothing. A failure shows "Something went wrong. Try again." and keeps the selection.

**In tests:** `test/features/reels/views/reel_selection_screen_test.dart` (the whole page with a real in-memory database), `view_models/reel_selection_test.dart`, `reel_picker_test.dart`, `reel_clip_actions_test.dart`; `flagForTest` in `test/support/reel_videos.dart` flags clips missing.

### Watching a clip (`lib/features/reels/`, `lib/platform/media_bridge.dart`, `lib/router/`, D-89)

**Getting there:** a tile on the reel's page does `context.push(Routes.clip(reelId, clipId))` (once however fast the taps: `OpenOnce`, a short window after the tap). The route (`/reels/:id/clip/:clipId`, a child of the reel's route) has `parentNavigatorKey: rootNavigatorKey`, so it is drawn on the router's root navigator **above the tab shell** (no nav pill) while the reel's page stays under it. Anything but a positive whole number for either id goes to `/reels`. Paths below are relative to `app/lib/`.

**Opening** (`features/reels/view_models/clip_viewer_controller.dart › ClipViewerController(reelId, clipId)`, watched by `views/clip_viewer_screen.dart › ClipViewerScreen`):
1. It awaits `reelByIdProvider(reelId)` and `reelClipsProvider(reelId)`, and **reads without awaiting** `reelClipTimesProvider(reelId)` (so the viewer opens at once; `times` is empty until `ClipTimeService.localTimes` answers).
2. The reel is gone → state `null`. Else the pager is the clips with `missing == false`, in the list's order (shot first, then id).
3. The clip to show is `_currentId ?? clipId`. Not in the pager: on the first build (`_opened` false) or with an empty pager → `null` (the tapped clip is missing, or not in this reel: **never another clip**). Otherwise (the clip on screen left the pager) → `min(_lastIndex, length - 1)`: the clip now in its place, the previous one if the last was left. A clip that turns missing while it is on screen (a verify) leaves the same way: the viewer moves to the neighbour, which then autoplays.
4. State `null` makes the screen, once and after the frame, `context.pop()` (or `go(Routes.reel(reelId))` when nothing can pop, a link). While the reel picker is up it waits: a pop then would close the picker, not the viewer; `_leaveIfGone()` runs when the picker has closed.

**Paging** (`ClipViewerScreen`): a `PageView.builder` (one page for each clip, keyed by the clip id). `onPageChanged` calls `goTo(index)`, which sets `_currentId` and the state's index; the ‹ › `TtStageArrow`s call `animateToPage` (`jumpToPage` when animations are off), and are disabled at the first and last page. When the index changes without a swipe (a clip left), `_followIndex` jumps the page controller to it after the frame. `onPageChanged` fires at the middle of a swipe, so it also sets `_outgoingClipId`: the page going out keeps its `_Stage` and player (it does not turn black while half on screen) until the pager's `ScrollEndNotification` clears it; the incoming page opens its player at once. In the same `onPageChanged` the outgoing page's player is **paused** (it keeps its frame), so two clips are never heard at once while a finger holds the swipe past the middle; a page swiped back to before the swipe settled still has its player, and it **plays again**. Neither opens a player (`ref.exists`). Each page's Remove (can't-play) removes **its own** clip.

**Playing** (`views/clip_viewer_screen.dart › _Stage`, `view_models/clip_player.dart › clipPlayerProvider(Uri)`):
1. The current clip's address is `clipVideoUri(clip)` (`platform/media_bridge.dart`): `null` for a missing clip, a MediaStore clip with `mediaStoreId <= 0` or no volume, a picked clip with no (or an unparsable) address; else `content://media/{volume}/video/media/{id}` or the picker address. `null` shows the can't-play state and makes no player.
2. Only the page showing watches `clipPlayerProvider(uri)` (the bottom scrubber watches the same one). Building it: `VideoPlayerController.contentUri(uri, VideoPlayerOptions(allowBackgroundPlayback: true))`, a `WidgetsBindingObserver` that pauses on `paused` and `hidden`, and `ref.onDispose` (observer removed, controller disposed). Then `initialize()`, the volume from `viewerMutedProvider`, and `play()` unless the observer says the app is in the background (Home pressed while the video was opening). An exception from `initialize` is the provider's error (logged by type) and the screen's can't-play state.
3. When the page changes the screen stops watching the old address: its provider is disposed (the controller with it) and the new page's is made. When the screen is popped the same happens. Mute: `viewerMutedProvider` (kept for the session) is followed by every live player.
4. The stage is the video fitted whole (`AspectRatio(value.aspectRatio)`); a tap plays or pauses (`value.isPlaying`); a play mark shows while paused. For a screen reader the stage is a button "Clip {i} of {n}" with the state "Playing" / "Paused" and the hint "Pause" / "Play". `value.hasError` (an error while playing) shows the can't-play state. The scrubber (`features/clips/widgets/clip_scrubber.dart › ClipScrubber`, shared with the tag player; it wraps `TtScrubBar`) shows the player's position and length and seeks with `seekTo`; before the player is open it shows 0:00 and the clip's own length.
5. The app going to the background pauses the video; coming back does not play it again.

**The header** (`TtPlayerHeader`): "{i} of {n} · {reel}"; under it the time where the clip was shot: `ttDayWeekdayText(wallClock)` · `wallClockTimeText(wallClock)` (the phone's 24-hour setting), then " · {city} time" if `!isPhoneZone && zoneCity(zoneId) != null`. Empty while the clip's `ClipLocalTime` is still being read; if reading the times **failed** (`ClipViewerState.timesFailed`) it shows the phone's own day and time (`dateTakenUtc.toLocal()`) with no city. `wallClock` is never converted (D-83). The "In" row is `clipReelNamesProvider(clipId)`: `watchReelIdsOf` and `watchAll`, names by reel id; while the next clip's names load, the row keeps the last names shown instead of disappearing for a frame.

**Add to reel / Move** (`_pickReels`): `pickAndPerform` (`views/reel_picker_sheet.dart`), the same function the reel page uses (see "Managing clips in a reel"), for one clip: `showReelPicker(mode, clipCount: 1, excludeReelId: reelId, perform)`, `ReelClipActions.addToReels` / `moveToReel`, the snack after the sheet has closed with **Undo**. `_pickerOpen` is set around the call. A move changes the reel's clips, so the controller applies step 3 of "Opening" and the viewer goes on.

**Share** (`_share`, Phase 8, D-126): the fourth action; `openShare(context, ref, [clipId])` (see "Picking clips and the editor sheet"), once however fast the taps (`_sharing`). The pager only holds clips that are on the phone, so Share is never off; a clip that went missing a moment ago is left out by the service and the snack says "These clips are missing from your phone.".

**Remove / Remove from reel** (`_remove`): `ReelClipActions.removeFromReel(reelId, {clipId})` → "Removed from {reel}. The video is still in your gallery." with **Undo** (`ReelSnacks.showUndoAll`) on the root messenger; a second tap while it runs does nothing; a failure shows "Something went wrong. Try again.". The viewer then follows step 3 of "Opening" (next clip, previous at the end, back when none is left). Undo works after the viewer has closed.

**Snacks:** `ClipViewerScreen` builds its own `Scaffold` (video theme) with the controls as its bottom bar, because the root messenger shows a snack in each Scaffold under it and the tab shell's is covered by this route.

**In tests:** `test/support/fake_video_player.dart › FakeVideoPlayerPlatform.install()` (no ExoPlayer; `uris`, `live`, `calls`, `failUris`, `failPlayer`, `hold`), `test/features/reels/views/clip_viewer_screen_test.dart`, `view_models/clip_viewer_controller_test.dart`, `view_models/clip_player_test.dart`, `test/platform/clip_video_uri_test.dart`, and the viewer routes in `test/router/app_router_test.dart`. A widget test that checks `platform.live` settles in real time after `pumpAndSettle` (`tester.runAsync(settle)`), because the disposal runs on the event loop.

### The tag player's session (`lib/features/tagging/view_models/`, D-96)

The view model behind the tag player (the screen is in "The tag player screen and All done" below). All in `tag_session_controller.dart › TagSessionController` (family on `int? startClipId`, auto-disposed with the route) unless named.

**Opening** (`build`):
1. Holds `clipsRepositoryProvider`, `reelsRepositoryProvider` and the `lastTagsProvider` notifier for the session's life (so an Undo after the player closed still works).
2. Listens to `ClipsRepository.watchInbox(pending)`. The first answer is the session: `inShootingOrder` (`domain/rules/shooting_order.dart`) of those clips, as ids, fixed; `TagQueue(session)` (`domain/rules/tag_queue.dart`, D-95). `index` is `startClipId`'s position, else 0; an empty session is `done`. `pending` is the session's ids, `clips` the rows by id, `scenes` = `groupIntoScenes(clips, {})` (phone time until the times come).
3. Without waiting: `ClipTimeService.localTimes(clips)` (D-83); when it answers, `times` and `scenes` are set again with the real times. A failure is logged by type.
4. Every later answer of the same stream goes to `_onPending`: `pending` = the session's ids still in it, the rows of those clips replaced (fresh identity, D-89), then `_followed`: if the clip at `index` is not pending and no write runs, `index` = `TagQueue.afterAction(index, pending)`, or `done` when null. Clips not in the session are ignored.

**One action at a time** (`_idle`): an action starts only when the player is still open (`ref.mounted`, checked first: reading `state` after dispose throws) and not closing (`leave()` was not called since the last `enter()`), the state is loaded, not done, no action runs (`_running`) and no chip is held (`confirmingReelId`); otherwise it returns null at once. It reads `session[index]` and `index` before awaiting anything.

**Tap a chip** (`tapReel(reelId, {reducedMotion})`):
1. `_idle` and not `multi`; the action begins; `confirmingReelId` = `reelId`.
2. `holdTimerProvider` (`Timer.new`) starts the hold: `tagHold` (`TtMotion.standardSet.medium4`, 400 ms) or `tagHoldReduced` (120 ms).
3. If the player closes in the hold, the screen calls `leave()` as the close starts (Close, See your reels, and Back or the predictive back through its `PopScope`; the route's exit transition outlasts the hold, and the session is disposed only after it): `_cancelHold` cancels the timer, completes the tap with null and ends the action, `confirmingReelId` is cleared, and `_idle` refuses every action from then on (Undo still runs). Nothing is written. `enter()` (the screen's `initState`) opens the session again for a player opened while the last one slides away, which shares it. `ref.onDispose › _cancelHold` still runs too. The same happens when the held clip (`_heldClipId`) leaves To tag before the hold ends (`_onPending`, D-96 point 8): the tap is dropped, `confirmingReelId` is cleared, a queued Undo may run, and the player moves on.
4. When the hold ends: `_tag(clipId, from, …)` → `_act`: `busy` on → `ReelsRepository.watchAll().first` (names; every chosen id must exist, else `StateError`) → `ReelClipActions.addToReels({clipId}, [reelId])` (the shared add of the reel pickers: it drops a clip that no longer exists, then one `ClipsRepository.addClipsToReels` transaction, D-84) → `LastTags.set` → `TagOutcome`. If a verify deleted the clip since the tap, the add has nothing to write: `_write` returns null (no `LastTags`, no count, no snack), `_act` ends, and the player moves on from the gone clip as it does for any clip that leaves To tag (minors batch, T4-m5).
5. `_advance`: `taggedPerReel` += 1 per reel, `index` = `afterAction(from, pending − clipId)` or `done`, `picked = []`, `multi` kept, `lastActedClipId` = the clip. Then `busy` off, `confirmingReelId` null, `_followed` (a change seen during the write), and the action ends. The screen shows the snack from the `TagOutcome`.

**Other actions** (each through `_idle`, then `_act`, so steps 4–5 apply):
- `confirmPicks()`: `addToReels({clipId}, picked ∩ existing reels)`, the reels in the order picked; a picked reel deleted meanwhile is left out, and with none left nothing is written and the dead picks are dropped. `setMulti`, `togglePick`, `longPressReel` (`multi` on with that reel) only change the state.
- `sameAsPrevious()`: the `LastTags` ids that are in `watchAll()`; none → nothing (null).
- `skip()`: `ClipsRepository.skipClips([clipId])` (`InboxUndo`, D-94); `skipped` += 1; `LastTags` untouched.
- `createReelAndTag(name, clipId:)`: waits for a running action, then `ReelsRepository.create(name)` (a name problem is thrown to the form, D-85); in Pick several the new id is added to `picked` (and if the clip showing left meanwhile, `_followed` moves on and clears the picks); else clip `clipId` (the one showing when the sheet opened) is tagged into it **if it is still waiting**, otherwise only the reel is made. Returns `(reel, outcome?)`, null only after the player has closed.
- `previous()` / `next()`: `TagQueue.previous/next(index, pending)`; `picked = []`, `multi` kept; at an end nothing moves.

**A failure** in `_act`: logged by type (ids only, D-10), `busy` off, `confirmingReelId` null, nothing moves, rethrown to the screen ("Something went wrong. Try again.").

**Undo** (`undo(records, clipId)`): works after the player closed too (the repositories and `LastTags` are held from `build`; `ref.mounted` is checked before `state`, and state updates are skipped). Waits for the state while it is still loading, then for any running action (`_running`); begins its own; `busy` on → `ClipsRepository.undoAll(records)` (last to first, one transaction, D-84) → `_goBackAfterUndo`: `watchInbox(pending).first` → if `clipId` is pending and in the session, `index` = its position and `done = false` → `watchReelIdsOf(clipId).first` → `_uncount` (what this session counted for those records, once, by identity, and only what is no longer true: a skip if the clip is back in To tag, a reel's +1 if the clip is no longer in it; `LastTags.undo` puts the previous last tag back unless a later tag replaced it). Then `busy` off and `_followed`. Only a failure of `undoAll` is rethrown (nothing moves; the snack says "Couldn't undo. Try again."); a failure in `_goBackAfterUndo`, after the records are written, is logged by type and the Undo is done. If the session could not load (`future` throws), the records are still put back.

**The player** (`features/reels/view_models/clip_player.dart › clipPlayerProvider(uri, use: ClipPlayerUse.tagging)`): as "Watching a clip" above, but the volume follows `tag_player_muted.dart › TagPlayerMuted` (kept alive, starts `true`, `toggle()`) and `setLooping(true)` is called after opening. `ClipPlayerUse.viewer` (the default) is unchanged. With `autoplay: false` (a family argument, default true) the player opens and is not started: the tag player asks with it off for the clip showing and the clip one ahead, and plays the one showing itself (D-97).

**In tests:** `test/features/tagging/view_models/tag_session_controller_test.dart` (in-memory Drift through `VmHarness`, `FakeHolds` for the hold, `SpyClips` to gate or fail `addClipsToReels`), `test/features/reels/view_models/clip_player_test.dart` (the tagging group).

### The tag player screen and All done (`lib/features/tagging/views/`, `lib/router/`, D-97)

The screen on top of the session above (paths relative to `app/lib/`).

**Getting there:** `context.push(Routes.tagFrom(clipId))` (`/to-tag/play?from=<id>`, or `Routes.tagPlayer` for the first clip); a tile on To tag and **Start tagging** do it (D-98, "To tag" above). The route is a child of `/to-tag` with `parentNavigatorKey: rootNavigatorKey` (above the shell, no nav pill, To tag kept under it). A `from` that is not a positive whole number is ignored. Close, Back and "Back to To tag" do `context.pop()`, or `go(Routes.toTag)` when nothing can pop. Each first calls `TagSessionController.leave()` (`_close`; system Back and the predictive back gesture through the `PopScope` around the screen, on `didPop`), as does **See your reels**: a chip's hold ends at once, not after the exit transition.

**Building** (`views/tag_player_screen.dart › TagPlayerScreen`, `build`): watches `tagSessionControllerProvider(startClipId)`, `reelSummariesProvider` (the reels, newest first, with cover and clip count), `thumbnailCacheProvider`, `tagPlayerMutedProvider` and `lastTagsProvider`. While the session is loading it shows black with Close; if it failed, also "Something went wrong. Try again." and **Try again** (`ref.invalidate` of the session). Loaded and not done:
1. `_noteClip` remembers which way the new clip slides in (from the end after a tag, skip or Next; from the start after Previous or Undo; none the first time).
2. It watches `clipPlayerProvider(uri, use: tagging, autoplay: false)` of the clip showing (for the scrubber), then `_preload`: `TagQueue.afterAction(index, pending − current)` is the clip the player goes to next; its `clipVideoUri` is held open, paused, with `ref.listenManual`, which is changed by a timer (`_aheadPoll`, 200 ms) after the frame and after the left clip's provider has gone (`_openAhead`, `ref.exists`, at most ten tries; a new clip ahead starts the wait again). At most two controllers live when settled; for a moment there can be three, going back or during rapid actions (Skip about every 100 ms keeps restarting the wait). A clip whose stage goes away is paused and rewound (`_PlayingState.dispose › _rest`), because it may still be held as the clip ahead.
3. The `Scaffold` (video theme, `resizeToAvoidBottomInset: false`): body = `TtVideoSurface` (no scrims: those are the viewer's) with `TtSegmentBar`, `TtPlayerHeader` and `_Stage`; bottom = `ClipScrubber`, the quick row (**Skip**, `TtSameAsPreviousButton`), `TtTagPanel` (the panel alone, at most 45 % of the screen's height, 40 % from `TtMetrics.largeTextScale` up). From that scale the header's subtitle is the range alone, allowed two lines (`wrapSubtitle: true`), and **Same as previous** may take two lines.
Done (and while the session loads): `_dropAhead` lets the clip ahead go (subscription closed, timer cancelled; Undo opens it again). Done shows `TagDoneView` (`TtTagDone`) with the reels' names, `taggedPerReel`, `skipped`, `sortedCount`; the bottom bar is gone.

**The stage** (`_Stage`, keyed by (clip id, address)): `clipVideoUri(clip)` is the only source of an address. No address → `_CantPlay`. Else it watches `clipPlayerProvider(uri, use: tagging, autoplay: false)`: loading → the clip's picture with a spinner; error → `_CantPlay`; data → `_Playing`, whose `initState` calls `play()` once (unless the app is in the background), so the clip showing plays and the one ahead does not; its `dispose` pauses and rewinds the clip, unless another stage still shows the same controller (`_PlayingState._stages`: a player opened while the last one slides away shares its controllers). A tap pauses or plays; `value.hasError` → `_CantPlay`. Behind it, the clip's thumbnail blurred (`TtMetrics.tagStage*`); at the sides, `TtStageArrow`s (`hasPrevious` / `hasNext`); a horizontal fling on `_SwipeArea` (a layer over the video, inset by `systemGestureInsets`; the video is not inset, and taps reach it through the layer) calls `previous` / `next` when it is fast enough (`kMinFlingVelocity`) and has travelled at least `_swipeMinDistance` (`kTouchSlop` × 4) from the touch-down. The new clip slides in (`_SlideIn`, `medium2`) unless animations are off.

**A chip** (`_panel` → `TtReelChip`): not in Pick several, a tap calls `_tapReel`: a haptic tick, then `TagSessionController.tapReel(reelId, reducedMotion: MediaQuery.disableAnimationsOf)`, which holds 400 ms (the chip is `done` while `confirmingReelId` is set; a held clip that leaves To tag cancels the hold, so the check never shows on another clip), tags, and returns a `TagOutcome`; `showTagOutcome` shows the snack. A long press is `longPressReel`; in Pick several a tap is `togglePick`; the footer is `confirmPicks`. **Skip**, **Same as previous** and the footer go through `_act`: it reads the snack's messenger, words and the session notifier **before** awaiting; null (ignored, or closed in the hold) shows nothing; an exception shows "Something went wrong. Try again.". The panel: Collecting chips, New reel, "Other reels ({n})" (open when no reel is Collecting, or the user toggled it) with the Ready and Published chips under it; no reels at all: the prompt and a full-width New reel.

**The snack** (`views/tagging_snacks.dart › showTagOutcome`): `ReelSnacks.showUndoAll(message, outcome.records, undo: () => session.undo(outcome.records, outcome.clipId))` on the root messenger. The message is `addedToText(outcome.reelNames)` or "Skipped". Showing it hides the one before, so only the newest offers Undo. Undo calls the held `TagSessionController` notifier: while the player is open the clip comes back and shows; after it closed the records are still put back (`ClipsRepository.undoAll`, one transaction). A failure shows "Couldn't undo. Try again.".

**New reel** (`_newReel`): `showReelForm(context, forClip: true, onCreateForClip)` through `OpenOnce`. Create and add: `ReelFormController.save(name, createWith)` checks the name (empty, too long, taken: told in the sheet), calls the player's `createReelAndTag(name, clipId:)` with the clip that was showing when the sheet opened, takes the reel it returns (no lookup by name), and the sheet closes; the snack is the tag's ("Added to {name}"), or "{name} created" when that clip had left To tag and only the reel was made. In Pick several the reel is picked instead and there is no snack. The name field's keyboard action is Done.

**In tests:** `test/features/tagging/views/tag_player_screen_test.dart` (in-memory Drift through `VmHarness`, `FakeHolds`, `FakeVideoPlayerPlatform` with `size`; the group "leaving during a hold, with the real timer" pushes the player as a page route over a stand-in page, with `Timer.new` and animations on, so the hold ends during the exit transition), `tag_player_golden_test.dart` (macOS images in `goldens/macos/`; skipped on CI), the form's tests in `test/features/reels/views/reel_forms_test.dart`, and the route in `test/router/app_router_test.dart`.

### Thumbnails (`lib/data/media/thumbnail_cache.dart`, `lib/features/clips/widgets/clip_thumbnail_image.dart`, D-74)

Nothing shows a tile yet (Task 10); `MediaLifecycle` calls `ThumbnailCache.prune()` once per app start ("App start and resume"). Paths are relative to `app/`.

**Showing one tile** (`Image(image: ClipThumbnailImage(clip:, logicalSize:, cache:), errorBuilder: placeholder)`):

1. `ClipThumbnailImage.obtainKey(configuration)`: `ClipThumbnailKey.of(clip, (logicalSize * (devicePixelRatio ?? 1)).round())`. A clip with no key (detached, no volume, a picked clip with no address, size below 1; a picked clip with an address has a key, see "Picked clips") makes the future fail with a `StateError`, which is the image error (step 8).
2. Flutter's image cache looks the key up (volume, number, `sizeBytes`, date taken, pixel size). A hit shows the decoded picture; no disk or bridge is touched.
3. A miss calls `loadImage`, a `MultiFrameImageStreamCompleter` with `scale: sizePx / logicalSize`, whose codec is `cache.load(clip, key.sizePx)` decoded by Flutter.
4. `ThumbnailCache.load(clip, sizePx)`: no key (same rule as step 1) gives null. Else `_inFlight[fileName]`: a load of the same key already running is joined; otherwise one starts.
5. Disk: read `<dir>/<fileName>`. Missing file or folder: miss. not starting with the JPEG marker `FF D8` (empty, one byte, foreign): delete it, miss. Otherwise set its modified time to `clock.now()` and return the bytes (no bridge call).
6. Bridge: `bridge.thumbnail(volume, mediaStoreId, sizePx, sizePx)` with a 15 second limit (Kotlin: `Thumbnails.load`, "Native bridge" above). Null, empty bytes, a timeout, a `PlatformException` or any `Exception` give null (logged as `No thumbnail from volume <name>: <code, `timeout` or type>`, nothing written; a timed-out load ends for everyone sharing it).
7. Write: create the folder if missing, write `<fileName>.tmp`, rename it to `<fileName>`. If that fails with an `Exception`, log by volume and exception type, delete the temp file, and still return the bytes. The entry in `_inFlight` is removed when the load ends, whatever the result.
8. Back in the provider: bytes are decoded; null bytes or a decode failure make the stream report an error (silent). On a decode failure of non-null bytes the provider first calls `ThumbnailCache.discard(clip, sizePx)` (deletes that key's file; nothing if a load of that key is running), so the next tile asks the bridge again instead of reading the same bad file. In both cases it schedules `ImageCache.evict(key)` so the failed key is not kept. The `Image` shows its `errorBuilder`, the placeholder.

**Pruning** (`ThumbnailCache.prune()`, once at app start from `MediaLifecycle`; never during a session):

1. List the folder (a missing folder: stop). For each file: a `.tmp` that this cache is not writing right now is deleted; a `.jpg` is `stat`ed (one that has vanished since the listing is skipped) and collected with its size and modified time; everything else (other names, sub-folders) is left alone.
2. Sort the `.jpg` files by modified time, oldest first (ties by path); `total` is their summed size.
3. For each file in that order: stop when `total <= maxBytes` (64 MB by default), else delete it and subtract its size.

**Providers:** `thumbnailCacheDirProvider` (kept alive): `getApplicationCacheDirectory()` + `/thumbs`. `thumbnailCacheProvider` (kept alive, a `Future<ThumbnailCache>`): `ThumbnailCache(ref.watch(mediaBridgeProvider), <that folder>, clock: ref.watch(clockProvider))`.

**In tests:** `test/data/media/thumbnail_cache_test.dart` uses a real temporary folder and `FakeMediaBridge`; its `renameFile` constructor parameter (`@visibleForTesting`) stands for a kill between the write and the rename, `listDir` lets a file vanish between the listing and the `stat`, and `bridgeTimeout` shortens the 15 seconds. `test/features/clips/widgets/clip_thumbnail_image_test.dart` resolves the provider with a 1x1 PNG at a pixel ratio of 3.

### Android unit tests (`tool/test_android`)

Run by `tool/check` (step 9c) and so by CI, or by hand: `tool/test_android`, with any extra Gradle arguments (`tool/test_android --tests 'app.taketray.media.VideoQueriesTest'`). See D-67.

1. `tool/test_android` › `cd`s to the repo root. If `app/android/local.properties` is missing, its `flutter.sdk` is not `$HOME/development/flutter` (the Flutter `tool/flutter` runs), or `app/android/gradlew` is missing (both files are git-ignored and written by the Flutter tool), it runs `(cd app && ../tool/flutter build apk --debug --config-only)`.
2. `cd app/android && ./gradlew :app:testDebugUnitTest --console=plain "$@"`. With `unitTests.isIncludeAndroidResources`, Gradle first runs Flutter's `copyFlutterAssetsDebug` (and the Dart build behind it), ordered before `packageDebugUnitTestForUnitTest` by a `dependsOn` in `app/build.gradle.kts`.
3. The tests in `android/app/src/test/kotlin/app/taketray/media/` and `.../background/`: `MediaAccessCheckerTest` is plain JUnit (the SDK level is an argument). The others use `RobolectricTestRunner` and run once per SDK level in `src/test/resources/robolectric.properties` (30, 33, 34, 35): `VideoQueriesTest` (selections, query arguments, `rowFrom` on a `MatrixCursor`), `VideoQueriesSqlTest` (the selections on real SQLite through a strict `SQLiteQueryBuilder`), `ZoneChangeStoreTest` (real preferences, and the receiver), `ZoneOffsetsTest` (`ZoneOffsets.offsetsMs`: London's summer, winter and clock-change instant, Kolkata, invalid ids, the length check, every zone against `java.util.TimeZone`), `ThumbnailsTest` (`FakeMediaProvider` registered for the media authority throws on open; it also records every query's uri, projection and arguments and answers with a scripted cursor), `VolumesTest` (injected version read, fake `Volumes`) and `MediaHostApiImplTest` (the implementation on a Robolectric `Activity`: the permission request and every blocked transition, intents, zones, the queries it sends and the rows it returns, chunking, unknown volumes, null cursor, thumbnail sizes, error messages). In `android/app/src/test/kotlin/app/taketray/background/` (D-103): `BackgroundStateStoreTest` (the store on `ProcessPrefs`, a test `SharedPreferences` whose "disk" only `commit()` writes, so a new store on `afterProcessEnd()` proves a write survived its process; round trips, corrupt values, the shade action's `atMs`, the posted count, and once on the real preferences), `BackgroundHostApiImplTest` (on a Robolectric `Activity`: stored before armed, the notification permission below and from 33 with every blocked transition, request codes, settings intents and their fallbacks, the phone maker), `LaunchActionsTest` (the allow-list, the history flag, the extra removed, a re-created and a restored activity, the relay, and the merged manifest: deep linking off, read with `PackageManager.getActivityInfo(..., GET_META_DATA)`; the `route` extra ignored), and for the new-clips job (D-104): `NewClipsCounterTest` (on `SqlVideoProvider`, a real SQLite video table behind the media authority that runs each Bundle selection through a strict `SQLiteQueryBuilder` and leaves out pending and trashed rows unless asked, as MediaProvider does), `NotifierTest` (the channels, the not-allowed cases, the flags, the public version, the PendingIntents through `ShadowPendingIntent`), `NewClipsJobServiceTest` (the real service through `Robolectric.buildService`, a direct executor and an injected `NewClipsJob`: posting, the same count, 0, off, not allowed, a thumbnail failure, errors, the zone, re-arming through the real `JobScheduler` shadow, `jobFinished` through `ShadowJobService`, and the merged manifest's service) and `ArmingTest` (the job's `JobInfo`, the identical-job check, off and no access, `Arming.of`, the channels, the alarms, `keepNewClips`); and for the reminders and re-arming (D-105): `ReminderTimesTest` (plain JUnit on `java.time`: later today, passed, London's spring and autumn changes, minute 0 and 1439, the clamp, the 10:00 rounding), `ReminderSchedulerTest` (the alarms through `ShadowAlarmManager`: type, time, allow-while-idle, inexact window, explicit broadcast; cancelling; the handled session), `ReminderReceiverTest` (the evening count on `SqlVideoProvider`, once a day, re-arm before posting, the copy and taps, the alarm fired as Android would; Still shooting?'s session, answered and once-per-session checks), `ShadeActionReceiverTest` (Turn off's generations, the job stopped and the new-clips notification kept; Keep on; another session; a button sent as Android sends it), `BootReceiverTest` (boot, update, clock and zone changes, the evening not twice after a reboot) and, in `.../app/taketray/`, `ManifestTest` (the receivers not exported, the filters, and no exact-alarm or foreground-service permission in the merged manifest). `tool/test_android -ProbolectricSdks=35` runs one SDK level only (a Gradle property that `app/build.gradle.kts` passes on as `robolectric.enabledSdks`), for quick iterations; `tool/check` always runs all four. Robolectric downloads each SDK's framework jar into `~/.m2/repository/org/robolectric` on first use.
4. The debug-only test media code (`android/app/src/debug/kotlin/app/taketray/`, D-77) is on the unit-test class path too, and its tests are in `android/app/src/test/kotlin/app/taketray/`: `TestMediaGuardTest` (plain JUnit: the delete rule as a pure function: folder and owner), `TestMediaTest` (Robolectric, on `FakeVideoStore`, an in-memory stand-in for the media provider that hides pending rows unless a query asks for them and records the order of `insert`, `write`, `update`, `delete`: pending-then-publish, the half-made row removed on a failed write, a row of another owner, the release app's file, a file outside the folder and a row with no owner all refused, leftovers) and `TestMediaHandlerTest` (the channel: arguments, replies, error codes, and that an error names no name or path). `DebugBackgroundHandlerTest` (Robolectric, Phase 6 Task 9: the `app.taketray/test_background` hooks `fireEvening`, `fireStill`, `runNewClipsJob`, `activeNotifications` and `pendingJobs` go through the real receiver, store, notification manager and job scheduler; the broadcast a hook sends equals the one the alarm carries; `pressStillTurnOff` sends the `PendingIntent` of the app's own Still shooting? Turn off action (the real receiver runs) and errors with `no-notification` or `no-action`, never pressing Keep on; `thermalStatus` reads the phone's thermal status; an error names the exception class only).
4a. The editors bridge (Phase 8 Task 4, D-121): `android/app/src/test/kotlin/app/taketray/editors/` (`CopyFolderPathTest` plain JUnit; the others Robolectric on `CopyFolderProvider`, a real SQLite video table behind a strict query builder that also answers `createDeleteRequest`) and `DebugEditorHooksTest`; `ManifestTest` reads the merged manifest's `<queries>` (see "Editors bridge and the copy folder guard").
5. Gradle exits non-zero on any failing test; reports are in `app/build/app/reports/tests/testDebugUnitTest/` and `app/build/app/test-results/testDebugUnitTest/`.

### Token build (`design/tools/build_tokens.py`)

Run by hand after changing a token: `python3 design/tools/build_tokens.py`. Paths in this flow are relative to the repo root.

1. `design/tools/build_tokens.py` (module level): builds the tonal palettes from the seeds, the colour roles for every brand option (`SCHEMES`) and picks the shipped one (`SHIPPED` → `LIGHT`, `DARK`, `PALETTES`); defines `TYPE`, `SHAPE`, `SPACING`, `ELEVATION`, `MOTION`, `SIZE`, `VIDEO_SURFACE` and `EXTENDED` (REC red, success green).
2. `build_tokens.py › main`: `parse_args` (argparse; an unknown flag, even an abbreviation of `--check`, exits 2 with a usage message before anything runs). Then `check_contrast` for the shipped brand and every option; any pair below its WCAG threshold prints `CONTRAST FAILURES` and exits 1 before anything is generated.
3. `build_tokens.py › generate`: clears `OUTPUTS` and runs the writers, each handing its files to `emit(path, text)`: `write_json` (`design/tokens/taketray.tokens.json`, `design/prototype/assets/tokens.js`, `design/tokens/colour-options/*.json`), `write_css` (`design/prototype/assets/tokens.css`), `write_kotlin` (`design/handoff/compose/*.kt`) and `write_dart` (`packages/taketray_ui/lib/src/tokens/tokens.g.dart`: `ttLightScheme`, `ttDarkScheme`, `TtColorTokens`, `TtTypeTokens`, `TtSpaceTokens`, `TtRadiusTokens`, `TtMotionTokens`, `TtSizeTokens`; shipped brand only).
4. Back in `main`: without `--check`, writes every entry of `OUTPUTS`; with `--check`, `stale_outputs` compares each with the file on disk, prints `stale: <path>` for each difference; then `orphan_outputs` lists files matching `GENERATED_DIRS` (`design/tokens/colour-options/*.json`, `design/handoff/compose/*.kt`, folders that hold nothing hand-written) that are not in `OUTPUTS` and prints `orphan: <path>` for each. Exits 1 if there is any stale or orphan output (0 otherwise, silently). Folders that mix in hand-written files (`design/tokens/`, `design/prototype/assets/`) are not scanned.
5. `packages/taketray_ui/lib/src/theme/take_tray_theme.dart › TakeTrayTheme.light / dark / video`: build (once, cached) `ThemeData` from `ttLightScheme`, or `ttDarkScheme` after `_oled` (tt-style.css §1), or that with `surface` = `TtColorTokens.videoBackground`. `_build` creates `TtThemeData.fromScheme(scheme)` (`tt_theme_data.dart`), which builds `TtColors.fromScheme` (`tt_colors.dart`, scheme roles + `TtColorTokens` + `TtMetrics` opacities) and `TtTextStyles.fromScheme` (`tt_text_styles.dart`, `TtTypeTokens` with the scheme's `onSurface`), and installs it as the theme's only extension, next to the Material component themes (values in `tt_metrics.dart`).
6. `packages/taketray_ui/lib/src/theme/tt_theme_data.dart › TtBuildContext.tt` (in any widget): `Theme.of(context).extension<TtThemeData>()` (throws a `FlutterError` if missing); when `MediaQuery.maybeDisableAnimationsOf(context)` is true, returns `copyWith(motion: TtMotion.reduced)`. `context.scheme` returns `Theme.of(context).colorScheme`.

### Release permission check (`tool/check_release_permissions`)

Run manually (slow: full release build), at the end of every phase. Not part of `tool/check`. See D-31, D-64.

1. `android/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml`: `<uses-permission INTERNET tools:node="remove"/>` (and the same for `ACCESS_NETWORK_STATE` and `WAKE_LOCK`, which the video player merges in, D-89) makes the manifest merger drop INTERNET from the merged release manifest; `src/debug` and `src/profile` manifests still declare it. Its `<application>` sets `allowBackup="false"`, `dataExtractionRules="@xml/data_extraction_rules"` and `fullBackupContent="@xml/backup_rules"`; the two files in `res/xml/` exclude every backup domain (D-64).
2. `tool/check_release_permissions` › start: `cd`s to the repo root, resolves the SDK (`$ANDROID_HOME` or `~/Library/Android/sdk`), picks the newest stable `build-tools/x.y.z` (version sort) that has an executable `aapt2`; if none, prints `No Android build-tools with aapt2 found in <dir>` and exits 1.
3. `tool/check_release_permissions` › `(cd app && ../tool/flutter build apk --release)`; exits on failure (`set -e`), then fails if `app/build/app/outputs/flutter-apk/app-release.apk` is missing.
4. `tool/check_release_permissions` › `aapt2 dump permissions` and `aapt2 dump xmltree --file AndroidManifest.xml` on the APK. An `awk` pass reduces the manifest dump to `application <attribute> <value>` lines and `permission <name> <maxSdkVersion|->` lines. If that pass yields no `permission` line or no `application` line, that is itself a problem (`no permissions were parsed from the manifest dump`), so a changed dump format cannot pass silently.
5. Backup: problems are collected when `allowBackup` is not `false` (including when it is missing), or `dataExtractionRules` or `fullBackupContent` is not set.
6. Forbidden permissions: a problem for each of `INTERNET`, `READ_MEDIA_IMAGES`, `MANAGE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE`, `ACCESS_MEDIA_LOCATION`, `ACCESS_NETWORK_STATE`, `WAKE_LOCK`, `SCHEDULE_EXACT_ALARM`, `USE_EXACT_ALARM` and `FOREGROUND_SERVICE` (D-105: the reminders are inexact alarms) found in the manifest (`<name> permission found in release build`), and again against `aapt2 dump permissions` (`... (aapt2 dump permissions)`), so a parse miss cannot hide one.
7. Required permissions (`require_permissions`, name:maxSdkVersion with `-` for none). The media ones (on by default since phase 3, Task 4; `ENFORCE_MEDIA_PERMISSIONS=0` switches them off for an experiment, D-68): a problem for each of `READ_MEDIA_VIDEO` and `READ_MEDIA_VISUAL_USER_SELECTED` that is missing, and for `READ_EXTERNAL_STORAGE` when it is missing or its `maxSdkVersion` is not 32. The background ones, always (D-101, D-105): `POST_NOTIFICATIONS` and `RECEIVE_BOOT_COMPLETED`, with no `maxSdkVersion`.
7a. The allow-list (final review M-5): the denylist above only knows the permissions we thought of, so every `<uses-permission>` name in the manifest must also be in `ALLOWED_PERMISSIONS` (the three media ones, `POST_NOTIFICATIONS`, `RECEIVE_BOOT_COMPLETED` and `app.taketray.DYNAMIC_RECEIVER_NOT_EXPORTED_PERMISSION`, which AndroidX adds for its own receivers). Any other, for example a `VIBRATE` or `USE_FULL_SCREEN_INTENT` that a plugin update merges in, is a problem (`<name> permission found in release build, and it is not in ALLOWED_PERMISSIONS`) that names it. A new permission is a decision: add it to the list and to its decision record.
8. Test-only code (D-77): `tool/check_release_permissions` unpacks every `classes*.dex` of the APK into a temporary folder and `grep`s them (no pipe: a `grep -q` at the end of a pipe can end early and fail the pipeline) for `TestMedia`, `test_media`, `DebugBackgroundHooks` and `test_background` (the background test hooks, Phase 6 Task 9), `quiet-rule`, and Phase 8's `DebugEditorHooks`, `test_editors` (D-121), `DebugCopyHooks` and `test_copy` (Task 5); each hit is a problem (`test-only code found in the release dex`). The scan first has to find `app/taketray/MainActivity` in the dex, otherwise that is a problem too (`the dex scan could not find ... so it cannot be trusted`), and no `classes*.dex` at all is a problem. R8 renames classes in release builds, so a class called `TestMedia` would be `lh` in the dex while the string `test_media` is never renamed: the script therefore also greps R8's `app/build/app/outputs/mapping/release/mapping.txt` (original names) for `TestMedia` when that file exists (`test-only code named in the release R8 mapping`). When it does not exist and the dex also lacks an app class that R8 would rename (`app/taketray/media/MediaHostApiImpl` by its real name), the class-name check could not see a renamed `TestMedia`, so that is a problem too (`no R8 mapping file ... the class-name check cannot run`) instead of a silent skip. The debug classes are in `src/debug` and the release and profile `DebugHooks` register nothing, so a clean build has neither.
9. If there are problems: prints the permission list, then one `RELEASE CHECK FAILED: <problem>` line each, to stderr, exit 1.
10. Otherwise prints the permission list, `No test-only code in the release dex (TestMedia test_media DebugBackgroundHooks test_background quiet-rule DebugEditorHooks test_editors DebugCopyHooks test_copy)` and `RELEASE PERMISSIONS OK`, exit 0.

**Plugins that add permissions** (D-89): `video_player` (ExoPlayer / media3) would add `WAKE_LOCK` and `ACCESS_NETWORK_STATE` to the merged release manifest. `AndroidManifest.xml` removes both next to `INTERNET` (item 1), and item 6 forbids them.

### Editing rules (`lib/domain/rules/{edit_folder_name,copy_file_names,space_check,byte_size,edit_split}.dart`, `lib/domain/models/{editor_app,clip_ref}.dart`, Phase 8 Task 2, D-119)

Pure functions; the editor tasks (3 to 13) call them. Nothing here reads a file, the clock or the zone.

1. **Folder:** `safeFolderName(reelName)` makes the reel's name a legal folder name under `Movies/TakeTray/` (forbidden characters to `-`, tab/LF/CR to a space, other controls, bidi controls and lone surrogates out, spaces collapsed, spaces and dots trimmed, cut to 200 UTF-8 bytes at a grapheme, empty to `Reel`). Task 6 then makes it unique (`uniqueFolderName`) and reserves it.
2. **Copy names:** `copyExtension(displayName, mimeType)` per clip, then one `copyFileNames(inShootingOrder, taken)` call per job (clip ids must be unique, else `ArgumentError`): `yyyy-MM-dd_HH-mm-ss.ext` from each clip's `ClipLocalTime.wallClock` (read by its fields, D-83), `_2`, `_3` for a clash with `taken` or with an earlier clip, compared without regard to case. The result is a clip id to name map that Task 6 saves in the job's plan (D-71).
3. **Space:** `spaceCheck(needBytes, freeBytes)` before a job starts: enough when `free >= need + 500 MB`; otherwise `shortByBytes` says how much more. The copy screen shows sizes through `byteSize`.
4. **Sizes:** `byteSize(bytes)` gives a unit and a number text (`900` MB, `1.3` GB), rounded half up before the unit is picked; any size above 0 is at least `1 MB`.
5. **The reel page while editing:** `splitForEditing` puts a clip in "In your edit" when it was sent or its copy is still in the folder, else in "New since you started editing". The add-later picker marks each live clip with `addPicks`: `fresh` (pre-ticked), `sentNotCopied` (selectable) or `inFolder` (nothing to copy).
6. **Finding a clip's video:** `ClipRef.of(clip)` is what both native sides (Tasks 4 and 5) get: volume and MediaStore id for a detected clip, the picker address alone for a picked one.

**In tests:** `test/domain/rules/{edit_folder_name,copy_file_names,space_check,byte_size,edit_split}_test.dart`, `test/domain/models/{editor_app,clip_ref}_test.dart`.

### Editors bridge and the copy folder guard (`lib/platform/editors_bridge.dart`, `pigeons/editors_api.dart`, `android/.../editors/`, Phase 8 Task 4, D-121)

The editor screens (Tasks 9 to 13) and `EditSessionService` (Task 6) call it through `editorsBridgeProvider`. Paths are relative to `app/`; Kotlin files are in `android/app/src/main/kotlin/app/taketray/editors/`.

**Making the contract:** as for the media bridge ("Native bridge" above), with `pigeons/editors_api.dart`, regenerated by `cd app && ../tool/dart run pigeon --input pigeons/editors_api.dart` into `lib/platform/editors_api.g.dart` and `EditorsApi.g.kt` (package `app.taketray.editors`, error class `EditorsError`, so it never clashes with Task 5's `copy_api.dart` in the same package). `tool/check_generated` keeps both fresh.

**Dart side:** `editors_bridge.dart › PigeonEditorsBridge` only converts: `ClipRef` to `ShareClipMsg` (volume, id, picker address; in the order given), `CopyFileRef` to `CopyFileRefMsg`, `SendResultMsg` to `SendResult`, the report's lists to sets (`DeleteReport`), `CopyFolderFileMsg` to `CopyFolderFile`, `InstalledAppMsg` to `InstalledApp`. A `PlatformException` passes through unchanged. The value types' `toString` print no folder, file or package name (D-10).

**Kotlin side:**

1. `MainActivity.configureFlutterEngine`: after the background bridge, `EditorsHostApi.setUp(messenger, EditorsHostApiImpl(this))`. `cleanUpFlutterEngine` unregisters it and calls `onHostGone`; `onActivityResult` hands every result to `editors.onActivityResult` (it takes only `DeleteRequests.REQUEST_CODE`, `0x7A75`); `onDestroy` calls `onHostGone`.
2. **Threads** (`EditorsHostApiImpl`): `apps`, `freeBytes`, `copyFolderNames`, `existingCopies`, `deleteCopies`, `filesInCopyFolders`, the launch-intent read of `openApp` and the prepare step of `requestDelete` run in `guardedOnIo` (Dispatchers.IO); the starts (`sendTo`, `shareWithChooser`, `openApp`, `openStoreListing`, the consent dialog) run in `guardedWith` on the main thread, where Pigeon calls them. An unexpected exception becomes `EditorsError("native-error", "<method> failed: <ExceptionClass>")`; `no-activity` when the activity is gone.
3. **Starting**: `starterNow` picks `ActivityStarter.replacement` (only the debug recorder sets it), else the starter given in tests, else `activity::startActivity`.
4. **`apps(packageNames, iconPx)`** (`EditorApps`): `iconPx` outside 1 to 512 is `IllegalArgumentException`. Per package, in order: `EditorApps.installedOverride[name] == false` gives not installed; else `getApplicationInfo(name, 0)`, `enabled`, then `getApplicationIcon(name)` drawn to an `iconPx` square ARGB bitmap and compressed to PNG. `NameNotFoundException` from either read gives `installed = false`; an icon that cannot be read or drawn gives `iconPng = null`; an override of `true` makes a missing app installed with no icon. Any other exception fails the call.
5. **`sendTo(package, clips)`** (`ShareIntents.send`): `uriOf` each clip (picker address when it is a `content:` URI with an authority; else `getContentUri(volume)` + id when the volume is not empty and the id is above 0; else left out, logged as a count). None left: `FAILED`, nothing starts. One: `ACTION_SEND` with `EXTRA_STREAM`; several: `ACTION_SEND_MULTIPLE` with an `ArrayList<Uri>`; type `video/*`, `setPackage`, ClipData of the same URIs in the same order (built with the constructor, no binder call), `FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION`. `ActivityNotFoundException` → `NOT_INSTALLED`; any other exception → `FAILED`.
6. **`shareWithChooser(clips, title)`**: the same URIs, `Intent.createChooser(sendIntent(null, uris), title)`, which copies the ClipData and read grant onto the chooser; true when started, false when nothing was left or the start threw.
7. **`openApp(package)`**: `EditorApps.launchIntent` (null when overridden away or `getLaunchIntentForPackage` has none) → false; else start it, false on any exception. **`openStoreListing(package)`**: `ACTION_VIEW` on `market://details?id=<package>` (`Uri.Builder`, the name is a query value); false when it cannot start; no web fallback.
8. **`freeBytes`**: `primaryFreeBytes()` = `StatFs(Environment.getExternalStorageDirectory().path).availableBytes`.
9. **The copy folder** (`CopyFolder`, on `MediaStore.Video.Media.getContentUri(VOLUME_EXTERNAL_PRIMARY)`; every query a `Bundle` with `_ID ASC`; a null cursor is `IOException`):
   - `existingCopies(refs)`: rows by `_ID IN (?,...)` in chunks of 500, pending and trashed excluded; the ids whose row passes `allowsDelete`, in ref order.
   - `deleteCopies(refs)`: the same read with pending and trashed included. Per ref: no row → `gone`; `allowsDelete` false → `refused` (no delete call at all); else `resolver.delete(itemUri, Bundle)` with the selection `relative_path = ? AND _display_name = ? AND owner_package_name = ?` (the row's own values) and pending and trashed matched: more than 0 → `deleted`, 0 → `gone` (it changed or went in between), an exception → `failed` and the next ref goes on. Logs the four counts.
   - `allowsDelete(path, name, owner, ref, ourPackage)`: the folder name is one plain segment (not empty, `.`, `..`, no `/` or NUL), `CopyFolderPath.isInFolder(path, folder)` (the whole path equal, ignoring ASCII case only, so a Kelvin sign or a dotless i is a different folder), `name == ref.fileName` exactly, and `CopyFolderPath.isOwn(owner, ourPackage)` (`context.packageName`).
   - `copyFolderNames()`: rows with `relative_path LIKE 'Movies/TakeTray/%'` (pending and trashed included, any owner); the rest of the path after the root (`CopyFolderPath.startsWithRoot`, ignoring ASCII case) up to its first `/`; distinct, sorted. `Movies/TakeTray Test/` never starts with the root.
   - `filesInCopyFolders()`: the same LIKE read without pending and trashed; only paths with exactly one segment after the root; `CopyFolderFileMsg(id, folder as stored, DISPLAY_NAME or '', SIZE or 0, isOwn)`.
   - `deletableUris(refs)`: rows (pending and trashed excluded) in their folder with their name, any owner (the guard without the owner check); their item URIs.
10. **`requestDelete(refs)`** (`DeleteRequests.run`): no activity → `no-activity`; already running (preparing or showing) → `busy`. On IO: `deletableUris`; none → false with no dialog; else `MediaStore.createDeleteRequest(resolver, uris).intentSender`. Then on the main thread `startIntentSenderForResult(sender, 0x7A75, ...)` and wait for `onActivityResult`: `RESULT_OK` → true, anything else → false. `onHostGone` fails a waiting request (or one still preparing) with `host-gone`; a result with nobody waiting, or another request code, is ignored. The system deletes the rows.

**Debug build** (`android/app/src/debug/kotlin/app/taketray/DebugEditorHooks.kt`, registered by `DebugHooks.register`): channel `app.taketray/test_editors`, handled by `DebugEditorHandler`: `setInstalledOverride(map or null)` sets `EditorApps.installedOverride`; `recordIntents(true)` sets `ActivityStarter.replacement = IntentRecorder()` and `false` clears it (and with it what was recorded); `takeRecordedIntents()` returns and clears the list. Each entry: `action`, `package`, `targetAction` (the chooser's target), `type`, `uris` (`volume:id` for `content://media/<volume>/video/media/<id>`, else `picker`), `grantRead` (on the chooser and its target both), `clipDataCount`, `data` (the `market://` address only). While recording, an intent for a package overridden as not installed throws `ActivityNotFoundException` and is not recorded. Wrong argument types answer `bad-args`.

**Manifest:** `<queries>` holds `<package>` entries for `com.lemon.lvoverseas`, `com.instagram.basel` and `com.frontrow.vlog`, beside Flutter's `PROCESS_TEXT` intent.

**In tests:** `test/support/fake_editors_bridge.dart › FakeEditorsBridge` (settable `installed`, `icons`, `sendResult`, `chooserResult`, `openAppResult`, `storeResult`, `bytesFree`, `folderNames`, `existing`, `deleteReport`, `files`, `requestDeleteResult`; records `calls`, `sent`, `chooserShares`, `openedApps`, `openedListings`, `deleteAsked`, `deleteRequested`; `failNext`). Without a set `deleteReport`, `deleteCopies` deletes the asked ids in `existing` and reports the rest gone. Dart: `test/platform/{editors_bridge,fake_editors_bridge,editor_packages_manifest}_test.dart`. Kotlin (Robolectric, `tool/test_android`): `editors/{CopyFolderPath,CopyFolder,ShareIntents,EditorApps,DeleteRequests,EditorsHostApiImpl}Test.kt` on `editors/CopyFolderProvider.kt` (a real SQLite video table behind a strict query builder, answering queries, deletes with a selection and `create_delete_request`), `DebugEditorHooksTest.kt` and `ManifestTest.kt` (the merged manifest's `<queries>`).

### Copying clips (`lib/platform/copy_bridge.dart`, `pigeons/copy_api.dart`, `android/.../editors/`, Phase 8 Task 5, D-122)

Copies a reel's clips into `Movies/TakeTray/<folder>/` in the app's own process, with no foreground service. Dart (Task 6) chooses the clips, names and folder and records each copy; Kotlin copies and reports. Paths are relative to `app/`; Kotlin files are in `android/app/src/main/kotlin/app/taketray/`.

**Making the contract:** `pigeons/copy_api.dart` → `lib/platform/copy_api.g.dart` and `editors/CopyApi.g.kt` (package `app.taketray.editors`, error class `CopyApiError`); `cd app && ../tool/dart run pigeon --input pigeons/copy_api.dart` (`tool/check_generated` covers it). Host API `CopyHostApi` (Dart calls Kotlin) and Flutter API `CopyFlutterApi.onCopyChanged` (Kotlin calls Dart).

**Set-up:** `MainActivity.configureFlutterEngine` makes `CopyHostApiImpl(this, CopyEngine.shared(applicationContext), send = CopyFlutterApi(messenger)::onCopyChanged)` and `CopyHostApi.setUp`; the impl attaches its listener to the engine. `cleanUpFlutterEngine` and `onDestroy` call `onHostGone()`: its listener is detached (only if still the attached one), the window is dropped, and the job goes on in the process-wide `CopyEngine`. Dart's `copyBridgeProvider` (kept alive) builds `PigeonCopyBridge`; the first listen to `changes` sets up the Flutter API, and `dispose` removes it.

**Starting a job:**

1. Dart: `CopyBridge.start(CopyRequest)` → `CopyRequestMsg` (each `ClipRef` as `CopySourceMsg`). A `busy` error becomes `CopyBusy`; any other `PlatformException` passes unchanged. **[bridge]**
2. `CopyHostApiImpl.start` → `CopyEngine.start`: under the engine's lock, `busy` if a job is set (running or a waiting result) or `deletePendingIn` is sweeping; `bad-request` (`requireSegment`) for a blank job id, a folder or file name with `/`, NUL, `.` or `..`, or a negative size. Then a `CopyJob` is made, one worker coroutine is launched on `Dispatchers.IO` (after any earlier worker has ended), and a first `running` event is posted, inside the lock and before the worker is launched, so no event of the worker can overtake it (`resume` does the same).
3. `CopyJob.run` → `copyItems`, per item from `next`: `CopyStore.availableBytes()` (StatFs of the primary volume) `< sizeBytes + 50 MB` → `spaceFull`; `insertPending` (pending row in the folder on `external_primary`); `openSource` (`openFileDescriptor(uri, "r")` on the clip's own volume or its picker address; length from `statSize`); `openTarget` (`"w"` on the row); 1 MiB chunks, the cancel flag checked before each, the debug throttle slept after each, a progress event when 250 ms have passed since the last event; the target closed (a failed close is a copy failure: nothing is published, step 4); bytes written compared with the length; `publish` (`IS_PENDING 0`, then `_ID` and `DISPLAY_NAME` read back). A copied or failed item moves `next` on and sends an event.
4. Failures: the original's (open, read, length) → row deleted, clip in `failedClipIds`, go on; no space (`isNoSpace`: "ENOSPC" or "No space left on device" in the message of the exception or a cause) → row deleted, `spaceFull`; any other copy failure → row deleted, `interrupted`; insert `IllegalArgumentException` → that item failed; any unexpected exception → `interrupted`. Under the lock, `run` then sets the end state, unless a cancel is waiting (step "Stop").
5. Every event goes through `post` to the main thread, where the attached listener (if any) sends `CopyJobMsg` to Dart with `CopyFlutterApi.onCopyChanged` (a failed send is logged by class name and dropped). Dart maps it to `CopyJob` (`copiedAt` UTC) on `changes`.

**Stop** (`cancel(jobId)`): unknown id → null. A running job gets the flag; the loop stops at its next chunk check (or after the file it is publishing), deletes its pending row, and then `deleteCopies` deletes every copy of this job with `deleteOwn(id, folder, name)` (copies deleted stay in `copied`, the rest move to `undeleted`); state `cancelled`. A job that is `finished`, `spaceFull` or `interrupted` gets a new worker that runs `deleteCopies`. A `cancelled` job is answered as it is. The call returns once the deletes are done.

**Resume and clear:** `resume` only from `spaceFull` or `interrupted` (and no cancel waiting): `running` again, a worker continues from `next`; `busy` while `deletePendingIn` sweeps, like `start`. `clear` forgets a result that is not running and not still deleting; another id does nothing.

**After a restart:** `current()` is null. `ownRowsIn(folder)` queries `relative_path = ? COLLATE NOCASE` with pending rows included, then keeps only rows that pass `CopyFolderPath.isInFolder` and `isOwn` (exactly `Movies/TakeTray/<folder>/`, owned by TakeTray). `deletePendingIn(folder)` (`busy` while a worker runs; `start` and `resume` are `busy` while it sweeps) deletes each own pending row with the selection `is_pending = 1`, so a row published meanwhile stays.

**The screen:** `keepScreenOn(on)` → `KeepScreenOn.set`: `addFlags`/`clearFlags(FLAG_KEEP_SCREEN_ON)` on the activity's window, on the main thread (posted there from another thread); nothing without an activity.

**Debug hooks:** `DebugHooks.register(engine, activity)` → `DebugCopyHooks.register`: channel `app.taketray/test_copy` with `setCopyThrottleMs(int)` (sets `CopyEngine.shared(...).throttleMs`; a negative or non-whole number is `bad-argument`) and `keepScreenOnFlag()` (the activity window's flag now).

**In tests:** Robolectric (`tool/test_android`): `editors/{CopyJobTest,CopyEngineTest,CopyStoreTest,CopyHostApiImplTest,KeepScreenOnTest}.kt` over `FakeCopyStore` and `FakeCopyProvider`, `DebugCopyHooksTest.kt`, `ManifestTest.kt`; Dart: `test/platform/copy_bridge_test.dart`, `test/platform/fake_copy_bridge_test.dart`; `test/support/fake_copy_bridge.dart` keeps the engine's rules for later tasks.

### Editing a reel (`lib/data/editors/edit_session_service.dart`, `lib/features/editors/view_models/copy_job_controller.dart`, Phase 8 Task 6, D-123)

`EditSessionService` (kept alive, `editSessionServiceProvider`) runs every editing session over `EditSessionsRepository`, `ClipsRepository`, `ReelsRepository`, `PhoneStateRepository`, `EditorsBridge`, `CopyBridge`, `ClipTimeService` and the clock. Every method that writes is queued on its own serial queue (`_serial`: each call runs after the ones queued before it, whatever they did), so two never interleave; inside a slot only private steps run. `editorChoices`, `share`, `jobContext` and `deleteOrphans` are not queued. Logs: ids, counts, bytes, the editor's enum name and error types (D-10).

**Editor sheet:** `editorChoices()` → `EditorsBridge.apps([capcut, edits, vn] packages, iconPx: 144)` → one `EditorChoice(editor, installed, icon)` each, in that order.

**Send to CapCut or Edits** (`sendOriginals(reelId, editor, clipIds)`, single-flight per reel, queued):
1. VN → `StateError`; a session of another editor → `StateError`.
2. `clipsByIds`, keep the ones not missing and in the reel (`clipIdsOfReel`), `inShootingOrder`. None → `SendOutcome(failed, 0)`, nothing written.
3. No session: `startSession(reel, editor, safeFolderName(reel name), alsoTaken: copyFolderNames())`.
4. `recordSent(reel, ids)` → the ids this call added.
5. `sendTo(editor.packageName, ClipRef.of each)`. `sent` → `SendOutcome(sent, n)`. `notInstalled`/`failed`/a throw → roll back: `endSession` if step 3 started it, else `removeSent(reel, added)`; a throw is rethrown.

**Share** (`share(clipIds, title)`): live clips in shooting order → `shareWithChooser`; none → false, no call. No session, no row.

**Plan a copy** (`planCopy(reel, editor, clipIds)`, queued): session checks (another editor → `StateError`; CapCut/Edits with no session → `StateError`); with a session, `_verify(reel)` (`existingCopies`; gone rows removed; a throw is logged and the rows stay); live clips of the reel with no recorded copy, in shooting order; `needBytes` their sizes; `freeBytes()`; `spaceCheck`; the folder: the session's, or `uniqueFolderName(safeFolderName(name), session folders + copyFolderNames())`; `startsSession` = no session.

**Start a copy** (`startCopy(plan)`, single-flight per reel, queued):
1. A saved `copyJobPlan` → `CopyBusy`, nothing written.
2. Clips still live, in the reel, without a copy; none → `StateError`.
3. First VN copy: `startSession(...)` (folder reserved, R1).
4. Names: `ClipTimeService.localTimes(clips)` → `CopyNameSource(id, wallClock, copyExtension(displayName, mime))` → `copyFileNames(..., taken)`, `taken` = the session's copy names + `filesInCopyFolders()` names in that folder (folder compared ignoring case).
5. Job id `<reelId>-<UTC ms>`. `PhoneStateRepository.saveCopyJobPlan(CopyJobPlan(jobId, reel, editor, folder, startedSession, items))` (refused while another job's plan is saved).
6. `CopyBridge.start(CopyRequest(...))`. Any failure in 3 to 6 → `removeCopyJobPlan(jobId)` and `_endIfEmpty(reel)` if step 3 ran; rethrow.

**While it copies:** `CopyJobController` (kept alive; `MediaLifecycle` builds it at start) listens to `CopyBridge.changes` and `EditSessionService.localJobs`; its state is the job. An engine event whose `(jobId, copied count, undeleted count, end state)` changed since the last apply → `applyJob(job)` (errors logged by type). An event of an older job id (smaller time), or of a job that ended for good, is ignored. `onResume()` reads `current()` and treats it like an event. `EditSessionService.clearedJobs` (a job id after `finishJob`, `cancelCopy`, or Done editing settled it) makes the controller drop that job: state null, so no screen opens for a job with no plan. `takeUnseenResult()`: true once per job id and end state, for a job not running; a resumed job keeps its id, so one that goes back to running is unseen again when it ends.

**`applyJob(job)`** (queued): ignored unless `job.jobId` is the saved plan's. Cancelled: the `copied` ids (deleted by the engine) are forgotten and their rows removed; `undeleted` go through the record step. Otherwise `copied` go through the record step: drop ids this process deleted or found gone; drop ones already recorded (same clip and id); a copy whose clip is not in the reel or whose reel has no session → `deleteCopies` (guarded), deleted/gone forgotten, one not deleted is recorded if the session exists; the rest → `recordCopies` one at a time (a `StateError` is logged by ids).

**`finishJob(jobId)`** (queued): the engine's job, if it is this one: running → `StateError`; else `_apply`. Then copies of plan items whose clip left the reel → `deleteCopies`, rows of deleted/gone removed. `clear(jobId)`; `_endIfEmpty` if the job started the session; `removeCopyJobPlan(jobId)`.

**`cancelCopy(jobId)`** (queued): `CopyBridge.cancel(jobId)`; an answer → `_apply` it and `clear`. No answer but the plan is this job's (recovered after a restart) → the recorded copies of its items → `deleteCopies`; deleted/gone rows removed; a `cancelled` job (deleted in `copied`, the rest in `undeleted`) on `localJobs`. Then `_endIfEmpty` if it started the session, and `removeCopyJobPlan`. A throw keeps the plan.

**`resumeCopy(jobId)`** (queued): the engine's job → `resume`; another engine job → `CopyBusy`. After a restart: the plan's items with no recorded copy whose clip is live and in the reel → `CopyBridge.start` with the **same** job id and the plan's names; none → a `finished` job on `localJobs` (the rest in `failedClipIds`).

**After Android stopped TakeTray** (`recoverAtStart()`, queued, once per start):
1. `current()` not null (the process lived): `_apply` it, end empty sessions except its reel and the plan's; null.
2. No plan: end empty sessions; null.
