3. `deletePendingIn(plan.folder)`, then `ownRowsIn(plan.folder)`: each finished row whose `fileName` is an item's → a `CopiedFile` for that item's clip at `storedNow()`. Session gone → those are deleted (`deleteCopies`), the plan removed, null. Else the record step (as in `applyJob`); a row matching no item is left alone.
4. The `interrupted` job from the plan, listing the items with a recorded copy (by clip), on `localJobs`; then sessions with no copies, no sent rows and no plan end.
5. Any throw: logged by type, nothing more changes, null (the plan stays).

**`jobContext(jobId)`**: the saved plan's `(editor, startedSession)` while it is that job's; else null.

**Done editing** (`doneEditing(reel)`, queued): a copy job of the reel that **runs** → `StateError`; any other job of the reel (finished and never continued, cancelled, `spaceFull`, `interrupted`, a plan left after a restart) is settled first (the engine's result applied and cleared, or, with a plan and no engine job, the folder recovered first so its finished copies are recorded and deleted too; then the plan removed and the job announced on `clearedJobs`, so `CopyJobController` drops it; an engine that will not clear the job yet, or a recovery that fails, throws and keeps the plan); no session → `StateError`; every copy → `deleteCopies`; deleted and gone rows removed; a copy the guard **refused** is let go (its row goes with the session, the file is left for the storage screen, `DoneEditingResult.leftBehind` counts it); any failed (or a throw) → `CopiesNotDeleted(count)` and the session and the rows not deleted stay; else `endSession`; `DoneEditingResult(editor, bytes of the deleted, leftBehind)`. The screen never has to call `finishJob` first.

**`verifyCopies(reel)`** (queued): `existingCopies` of the recorded copies; the gone rows removed (and forgotten); a throw changes nothing and is rethrown.

**Before clips leave a reel** (`beforeClipsLeaveReel(reel, ids)`, queued): no session → 0; those clips' copies → `deleteCopies`; deleted/gone rows removed; returns how many; any left → `CopiesNotDeleted`. Callers: `ReelClipActions.removeFromReel` and `moveToReel` (source reel), `ReelDetailController.removeMissing`.

**Before a reel is deleted** (`beforeReelDelete(reel)`, queued): the engine's job for this reel, or the saved plan's → `_cancel`; then every copy → `deleteCopies` (any left → `CopiesNotDeleted`, nothing ended); `endSession`. Caller: `ReelActions.confirmDelete`, before `ReelsRepository.delete`.

**Storage** (`storage()`, queued): `summaries()`; `filesInCopyFolders()` (a throw → no orphans, logged); files whose id is no session's copy, minus the folders of the saved plan and the engine's job, grouped by folder → `OrphanFolder(folder, count, bytes, refs)`, folders sorted ignoring case. **`deleteOrphans(folder)`**: `requestDelete(files)` → the folder's bytes, or 0 when declined.

**The plan row:** `PhoneStateRepository.copyJobPlanKey = 'copyJobPlan'`, JSON `{jobId, reelId, editor, folderName, startedSession, items: [{clipId, fileName, sizeBytes}]}`; an unreadable value is none (key name logged); `saveCopyJobPlan` refuses another job's plan (`StateError`); `removeCopyJobPlan(jobId)` is compare-and-delete. Not exported; an import deletes it.

**In tests:** `test/data/editors/edit_session_service_test.dart` (in-memory database, `FakeEditorsBridge`, `FakeCopyBridge`, a fixed clock; a "restart" is a new service with a new `FakeCopyBridge` keeping the folder rows), `test/features/editors/copy_job_controller_test.dart`, `test/support/spy_edit_session_service.dart` (the real service noting the reel hooks), and `VmHarness`, which now overrides both bridges with fakes (`h.editors`, `h.copy`).

### Picking clips and the editor sheet (`lib/features/editors/`, `lib/router/`, Phase 8 Task 9, D-126)

**Getting there:** the reel page's **Edit video** button or the selection ⋮ → **Edit video** does `context.push(Routes.editSelectOf(reelId, clipIds: …))` (`/edit?reel=ID[&clips=ID,ID][&add=1]`, `editSelectRoute()`: a top-level route on the root navigator, so no nav pill; no positive `reel` → Reels).

**The picking screen** (`views/edit_select_screen.dart › EditSelectScreen`, `view_models/edit_select_controller.dart › EditSelectController(EditSelectArgs(reelId, preselected))`):
1. The controller awaits `reelByIdProvider` and `reelClipsProvider` and reads `reelClipTimesProvider` without awaiting (as the reel page, D-87). No reel → state `null` → the screen does `context.go(Routes.reels)` once after the frame. Else the live clips (`!missing`) are `groupByShootingDay`'d.
2. The choice (`_selected`) is seeded once with `preselected ∩ live ids`; every later build keeps it ∩ live ids, so a clip that goes missing or leaves the reel is un-ticked and the rest stays ticked. `toggle(id)` ignores an id that is no live clip; `selectAll()` ticks every live clip; `clear()` none. The state carries the ticked ids, `pickedInOrder` (`inShootingOrder`, oldest date taken first, then id), `pickedDurationMs`, `pickedBytes` and `liveCount`.
3. The screen: `TtAppBar("Pick clips to edit", reel name, close, **Select all** / **Clear** (`allPicked`; off with no live clip))`, `TtInfoBar`, per day a `TtDayHeader("Day {n} · {date}", "{k} clips")` and a lazy 3-column `SliverGrid` of `TtClipTile`s (always picking: `selected` is a bool; the time where it was shot, the length, the horizontal mark; no layers badge), and `TtBottomBar` ("No clips picked" / "Tap the clips you want to edit"; "{n} clips · {m:ss}" / "{size} · in shooting order" with `byteSizeText`) with **Continue** (off at zero).
4. **Continue** → `showEditorSheet(context, reelId:, clipIds: pickedInOrder)` behind a `_sheetOpen` flag (one sheet however fast the taps).
5. **Add mode** (`&add=1` → `EditSelectArgs(reelId, preselected, addMode: true)`, D-128; the banner's **Add more clips** and the page's **Copy to VN folder**): the controller also awaits `reelEditSessionProvider(reelId)` and runs `addPicks(live ids, sentIds:, liveCopyIds:)`. `addEditor` is the session's editor (null without one); `inFolder` the `AddPick.inFolder` ids. The choice is seeded once with the preselected ids and the `fresh` ones; `_set` always drops `inFolder` from the user's ticks, and `selected` is the ticks ∪ `inFolder`, so a folder clip is ticked and locked (toggle, Clear and Select all leave it), and a copy forgotten later frees it unticked. `toCopyInOrder` / `toCopyBytes` are the picked clips without `inFolder`; `allPicked` needs every non-folder live clip ticked and at least one. The screen: `TtAppBar("Add clips to VN folder", reel name)`, a folder tile with `vnLabel` "In VN folder" and the label "…, already in VN folder" whose tap (or long press) shows "Already in the VN folder. Copies are deleted when you tap Done editing." (`ReelSnacks.show`, no check mark), and `_addBar`: "{n} new clips to copy" / "{size} · copies are added, never renamed", or "Pick clips to add" / "{n} already in the VN folder", with **Copy** → `push(Routes.copySpaceOf(reelId, vn, toCopyInOrder))` through an `OpenOnce`. A state whose `addEditor` is not VN (no session, or CapCut or Edits until Task 12) → `_close()` once after the frame (pop, or `go(Routes.reel(id))`).

**The editor sheet** (`views/editor_sheet.dart`, `view_models/editor_choices.dart`):
1. `editorChoicesProvider` (auto-dispose) → `EditSessionService.editorChoices()`; a throw → all three installed, no icon (logged by type). Rows: `TtListRow(tall)` with `TtAppIcon` (letters C, E, VN) or `TtRowIcon(apps)`; an editor not installed is `off` with "Not installed" and `semanticLabel` "{name}, not installed"; the note "Editors you don't have are greyed out." shows when any of the three is off. The subtitle's length and VN's size come from `reelClipsProvider(reelId)` for the asked ids.
2. A row sets `_busy` (every row is inert, `PopScope(canPop: !_busy)`) and:
   - **VN** → pops `_OpenVn`; `showEditorSheet` then does `router.push(Routes.copySpaceOf(reelId, vn, clipIds))`. **Other app…** → pops `_OpenShare`; then `shareClips`.
   - **CapCut / Instagram Edits** → `EditSessionService.sendOriginals(reelId, editor, clipIds)`. `sent` → pops `_Sent(editor, n)`; `showEditorSheet` then leaves the picking screen (`router.pop()` when `canPop`, else `go(Routes.reel(reelId))`), shows "Sent {n} clips to {editor}" on the root messenger and starts `_offerReady`. `notInstalled` → `showEditorMissingDialog` (the sheet is under it), then `invalidate(editorChoicesProvider)` and the rows work again. `failed` or a throw (logged by type) → `SheetFailure` "Couldn't open {editor}. Try again." in the sheet footer; `_busy` off.
3. `_offerReady(navigator, container, reelId)`: `Future.delayed(1300 ms)`; if the root navigator is mounted, re-reads the reel; only `ReelStatus.collecting` goes on: `showMarkStatusDialogIn(navigator.overlay.context, container, reelId, ready)`. Everything is in a try/catch that logs the type: the app may be gone.
4. `showMarkStatusDialogIn` (`views/mark_status_dialog.dart`): reads the reel (gone or already that status → nothing), `showTtDialog` ("Mark {name} as {status}?" with the status icon, "Not now" / "Mark as {status}"); the confirm calls `ReelActions.setStatus` and shows `reelStatusChangedSnack` ("Marked as {status}"); a failure shows "Something went wrong. Try again.". `showMarkStatusDialog(context, ref, …)` is the same through `ProviderScope.containerOf(context)`.
5. `showEditorMissingDialog(context, editor)` (`views/editor_missing_dialog.dart`): "{editor} isn't installed" / "Install {editor} from the Play Store, or choose another editor."; **Open Play Store** → `EditorsBridge.openStoreListing(package)` (a false result or a throw: nothing more); **Choose another** closes.

**Share** (`views/open_share.dart`): `openShare(context, ref, ids)` looks up the snacks, the words and `editSessionServiceProvider` before any `await`, then `shareClips`: `service.share(ids, "Share video" / "Share {n} videos")`; `false` → "These clips are missing from your phone."; a throw → "Something went wrong. Try again."; empty `ids` → nothing.

**In tests:** `test/support/pump_screen.dart` has stub routes at `/edit` (`editSelectRouteMarker`) and `/edit/space` (`copySpaceRouteMarker`); `FakeEditorsBridge` has `hold('sendTo' | 'shareWithChooser')` and `release(...)` to keep a call running while a test taps again, and (as always) the harness's editors fake answers nothing until a test opts in (`h.editors.allow({'apps', 'sendTo', 'copyFolderNames'})`). `test/features/editors/` holds `edit_select_controller_test`, `edit_select_screen_test`, `editor_sheet_test` (the sheet, the sends, VN, Other app…, the missing dialog), `mark_status_dialog_test` and `editors_golden_test` (macOS only; `tool/flutter test --update-goldens test/features/editors/editors_golden_test.dart`).

### The VN copy screens (`lib/features/editors/`, `lib/router/`, `lib/app.dart`, Phase 8 Task 10, D-127)

**Getting there:** the editor sheet's **VN** row does `router.push(Routes.copySpaceOf(reelId, vn, clipIds))`. `copyRoutes()` (`app_router.dart`) registers three top-level routes on the root navigator (no nav pill): `/edit/space?reel=&editor=&clips=` (`CopySpaceScreen`), `/edit/copying?job=` (`CopyProgressScreen`) and `/edit/ready?reel=` (`CopyReadyScreen`). A reel that is not a positive whole number, an editor that is not an `EditorApp` name, no clip ids or an empty job id redirect to Reels.

**The space check** (`views/copy_space_screen.dart`, `view_models/copy_space_controller.dart › CopySpaceController(CopySpaceArgs(reelId, editor, clipIds))`):
1. The controller awaits `reelByIdProvider` (no reel → `null` → the screen does `context.go(Routes.reels)` once after the frame) and calls `EditSessionService.planCopy`. `CopySpaceState`: `enough` (`plan.space.enough`), `nothingToCopy` (`plan.clipIds` empty), `needFraction = need / free` (1 with no free space) and `haveFraction = (free − 500 MB) / need`, 0 when `free ≤ 500 MB` or `need ≤ 0`, at most 0.999999.
2. **Enough:** `TtAppBar("Copy clips for VN", close)`; "These {n} clips need {size}" (one clip: "This clip needs"), "You have {free} free."; `TtStorageBar.enough` ("Copies: {size}", "Free after copying: {freeAfterBytes}", semantics "Free space {free}, copies need {need}"); "TakeTray keeps 500 MB free for your phone."; `TtCallout` "Why copies?" (`richBody`: `boldSpans(vnCopyWhyBody(boldMark('Movies/TakeTray/<plan.folderName>')))`) and "Copies are temporary"; **Copy {n} clips** (large, `content_copy`) and **Back**. **Short:** "Not enough space", "These {n} clips need {size}. You have {free} free.", `TtStorageBar.short(haveFraction, "You have {free}", "Short by {shortByBytes}")`, the reserve note, a warning callout "What you can do", **Select fewer clips** (`pop`, else `go(reel)`) and **Cancel** (`go(Routes.reel)`, no snack). **Nothing to copy** and a plan that threw ("Couldn't start copying. Try again." with **Try again** = `ref.invalidate`, and **Back**) are `TtStatusLayout`s.
3. **Copy** sets `_starting` (both buttons and the system back are inert), calls `CopySpaceController.start(plan)` = `EditSessionService.startCopy`, and on success `router.pushReplacement(Routes.copyProgressOf(jobId))`. Any throw (`CopyBusy`, a bridge error, `StateError` for clips gone) clears `_starting`, shows a warning callout "Couldn't start copying. Try again." and leaves the screen; the service has taken back its writes.

**The progress screen** (`views/copy_progress_screen.dart`, `view_models/copy_stage.dart › copyStageOf(job)`, `copyFailedCount(job)`):
1. `copyStageOf`: `running`, `spaceFull`, `interrupted`, `cancelled` are their own; `finished` is `finished` with no failed clip, `allFailed` when `failedClipIds.toSet().length ≥ total`, else `someFailed`.
2. State: `initState` adds the job id to `OpenCopyScreens`, listens to `copyJobControllerProvider` and to `reelByIdProvider(reelId)` (the reel id is the job id's part before the last `-`), reads `jobContext` and, after the first frame, calls `_react` with the current job.
3. `_react(job)`: no job, or another job's id → `_gone`; else it marks the job seen, sets the window flag for the stage (`_syncScreen`), and for a clean `finished` job (when no action is running, no dialog is open and no exit is pending) calls `_finish`. `_gone` does nothing while our own stop or finish runs; for a null job never seen it reads the engine once (`CopyJobController.onResume`) and reacts again; otherwise `_goOut()`.
4. **The window flag** (`_setScreen`): asks `CopyBridge.keepScreenOn(on)` only when the value changes (`force` on `AppLifecycleListener.onResume` while `running`); on for `running` only; off for every other stage, when `_stop`/`_finish` start, in `_goOut` and in `dispose`. A throwing call is logged by type.
5. **Views:** `running`, `finished` and `cancelled` show the copying view (`TtStatusLayout(folder_copy, primary)`, `TtProgressBar(value: done / total, label: "{done} of {total} clips", semanticLabel, percentText)`, "{done} of {all} · into Movies/TakeTray/{job.folderName}", the keep-open line, **Cancel** outlined, or **Try again** when an exit is pending); `spaceFull` ("Phone storage is full", `sd_card_alert`, error) and `interrupted` ("Copying stopped", `warning`) offer **Try again** (`resumeCopy`) and **Delete copies and stop** (`_stop`); `someFailed` ("{n} clips couldn't be copied", the failed clip's time from `failedClipTimeProvider` for one clip) offers **Continue with {m} clips** (`_finish`) and **Cancel** (`_stop`); `allFailed` offers **Back** (`_stop`).
6. **Cancel**, and the system back (`PopScope(canPop: false)`), call `_askStop`: `showStopCopyDialog(copies: job.copied.length)` → true only for **Stop**. The all-failed screen's back is `_stop` directly.
7. **`_stop`:** `_working` on, window flag off, `jobContext` read, `EditSessionService.cancelCopy(jobId)`, then `_goOut(snack: startedSession ? "Copying stopped. No copies were kept." : "Copying stopped. The new copies were deleted.")`. **`_finish`:** `jobContext`, `finishJob`, then (first copy) `router.pushReplacement(Routes.copyReadyOf(reelId))` or (add) `_goOut(snack: "{n} clips added to the VN folder")`, `n = job.copied.length`. **`_resume`:** `resumeCopy`, then `_react`. A throw logs the type, shows "Something went wrong. Try again." under the words (`_error`), turns `_working` off, keeps the screen, and (stop where the engine did cancel, or finish) sets `_pending` so the copying view's button reads **Try again** and repeats it.
8. **`_goOut`:** once; `_left`, window flag off, then after the frame `router.go(Routes.reel(id))` (`Routes.reels` when the reel is gone or the id is bad) and the snack on the root messenger.

**The ready screen** (`views/copy_ready_screen.dart`, `view_models/copy_ready_controller.dart › CopyReadyController(reelId)`, `editStateOfProvider`): `CopyReadyState(reelGone, session, clips)` — the clips with a recorded copy that are not missing, in shooting order. The screen: close, check circle + "Ready for VN", `boldSpans(vnReadyIntro(boldMark("New project"), boldMark(folder)))`, `TtIllustratedSteps(newProject, folders [Camera, Download, <folder>, WhatsApp Video] highlighted 2, tiles (first 6, `clipThumbnail`))`, **Open VN** (`EditorsBridge.openApp`; `false` → `showEditorMissingDialog`; a throw → "Something went wrong. Try again." snack; one at a time) and **Done**; Done, close and the system back `go(Routes.reel)`. No session → the reel's page; no reel → Reels.

**Results nobody has seen** (`views/copy_result_opener.dart › CopyResultOpener`, in `TakeTrayApp.builder` inside `LaunchActionsListener`): `ref.listenManual(copyJobControllerProvider)` → `_check()`: a running job does nothing; any other state asks `takeUnseenResult()` and, when true and no progress screen for the job is open (`OpenCopyScreens.isOpen`), pushes `Routes.copyProgressOf(jobId)` after the frame. At the first frame and on every `AppLifecycleListener.onResume` it first awaits `CopyJobController.onResume()`, then `_check(openRunning: true)`, which also opens a job that still runs (once per job id per app run, and not when its screen is open).

**In tests:** `test/features/editors/{copy_stage,copy_space_screen,copy_progress_screen,copy_ready_screen,copy_golden}_test.dart`, `test/app/copy_result_opener_test.dart`, `test/router/app_router_test.dart` (the three routes), with `copy_support.dart` (`reelWithClips`, `startVnJob` → `VnJob.report(...)` builds engine snapshots, `leaveAndReturn`), `FakeCopyBridge.screenOnCalls` and `pumpScreen(copyScreens: true)`.

### Error logging

Installed once at start-up by `runAppWithLogging`; `main` calls it in Task 7 of phase 0 and runs the app inside `body`. It never waits for the log directory before starting the app.

Setup:
1. `core/logging/logging_setup.dart › runAppWithLogging`: sets `Logger.root.level = INFO` and subscribes to `Logger.root.onRecord`. Until the sink exists, each record is formatted and held in an in-memory list (max 200); once logging is disabled (step 5) nothing is buffered.
2. Same function: sets `FlutterError.onError` (also `presentError` in debug mode; its record's message is the private `_frameworkErrorTag`, "Flutter framework error") and `PlatformDispatcher.instance.onError` to call `Logger('crash').severe(message, error, stack)`.
3. Same function: enters `runZonedGuarded` (its error handler also calls `Logger('crash').severe`), calls `WidgetsFlutterBinding.ensureInitialized()` inside it, and starts `_attachSink` without awaiting it.
4. Same function: runs `body` immediately, so start-up errors thrown by `body` land in the buffer through step 2/3.
5. `_attachSink` (concurrently): awaits `logDir()` (`path_provider` application support directory, passed in by `main`) with a 5 s timeout. On success it creates the `LogFileSink`, writes the buffered lines in order, switches the subscription to write directly and calls `onSinkReady(sink)` (a throwing callback is ignored). If `logDir()` throws or times out it drops the buffer, disables buffering and prints one `debugPrint` line.

A log record (any `Logger('x').info/warning/severe`):
1. `package:logging › Logger.root.onRecord` delivers the `LogRecord` to the subscription from step 1 above.
2. `core/logging/logging_setup.dart › formatRecord`: `2026-10-24T09:12:03.120Z LEVEL logger: message`, plus indented `error:` and `stack:` lines when present. Message and error text go through `_redact` (paths, URIs become `<path>`; a `FileSystemException` is rendered without its path). `_describeError` rebuilds database errors from their fields: `SqliteException(<code>): <message>` with no statement or values, a `DriftRemoteException` as its cause, an `InvalidDataException` as its failing columns, `CouldNotRollBackException` and `DriftWrappedException` by their causes; any other text containing `Causing statement` is cut there (`<statement removed>`), stack traces are kept as they are, and a record over 8 KiB is cut with `…[truncated]`.
3. Same listener, debug builds only (`kDebugMode`): `debugPrint`s the formatted line to the console, unless the record is a framework error (its message object is `_frameworkErrorTag`; `presentError` already printed it). Release builds skip this step. The line then goes to the sink if it exists, otherwise to the buffer (or nowhere once logging is disabled).
4. `core/logging/log_file_sink.dart › LogFileSink.write`: chains onto the previous write (writes never interleave), reads the current file size once, then:
   1. if the line would push `taketray-0.log` past `maxBytes` (512 KiB), `_rotate` deletes `taketray-1.log` and renames `taketray-0.log` to `taketray-1.log`;
   2. appends the line to `taketray-0.log` and flushes.

An uncaught error:
1. Flutter framework error → `FlutterError.onError` (prints it to the console with `presentError` in debug); async error outside the framework → `PlatformDispatcher.onError` (returns `true`, handled); error escaping `body`'s zone → the `runZonedGuarded` handler.
2. Each calls `Logger('crash').severe(...)`, which continues at "A log record" step 1; in debug builds the zone and platform errors reach the console at "A log record" step 3.

Reading the log: `LogFileSink.readAll` returns `taketray-1.log` then `taketray-0.log` (oldest first); `LogFileSink.exportFile` writes that text to `taketray-log.txt` for the Settings share action (phase 10, using the sink from `onSinkReady`).

### On-device test (Patrol)

Run by hand on a connected phone (`docs/dev-setup.md`); not part of `tool/check`.

1. `tool/patrol test -d <device>` (all tests in `patrol_test`; add `-t patrol_test/<file>.dart` for one): puts the pinned Flutter (`~/development/flutter/bin`) and the Android SDK `platform-tools` first on `PATH`, sets `PATROL_ANALYTICS_ENABLED=false` (analytics off for this run; see `docs/dev-setup.md` for the config file), `cd`s to `app/`, inserts `--no-uninstall` after `test`/`develop` unless the caller passed `--uninstall` or `--no-uninstall`, and runs `patrol_cli` (`~/.pub-cache/bin/patrol`) with `--flutter-command` set to the pinned `flutter`. The generated `patrol_test/test_bundle.dart` (git-ignored) is deleted when the command exits, so `tool/check` never sees it.
2. `patrol_cli`: reads the `patrol:` block of `pubspec.yaml` (`app_name: TakeTray`, `android.package_name: app.taketray.dev`), generates `test_bundle.dart` from the requested test file and has Gradle build the debug app APK plus the `androidTest` APK. The debug build type adds `applicationIdSuffix = ".dev"` and the launcher label "TakeTray Dev" (`manifestPlaceholders["appLabel"]`, read by `android:label="${appLabel}"` in the manifest), so the app Patrol installs and tests is a second app next to the owner's `app.taketray` (D-52); release and profile builds keep `app.taketray` and "TakeTray".
3. `android/app/build.gradle.kts` › `defaultConfig.testInstrumentationRunner`: the test APK uses `pl.leancode.patrol.PatrolJUnitRunner`; `testOptions.execution = ANDROIDX_TEST_ORCHESTRATOR` runs each test in a fresh process through the `androidTestUtil` orchestrator (`clearPackageData` wipes the data of `app.taketray.dev` between tests, never that of `app.taketray`).
4. **[bridge]** `android/app/src/androidTest/java/app/taketray/MainActivityTest.java › testCases`: starts `MainActivity` through the runner, waits for the Patrol app service in the app process and lists the Dart tests; `runDartTest` then runs one named test.
5. `patrol_test/app_shell_test.dart › main`: `patrolTest` pumps `ProviderScope(child: TakeTrayApp())`, asserts the Reels screen shows "Reels", taps "To tag" then "Settings" inside the `TtNavPill` (`$(TtNavPill).$('To tag')`, by label text) and asserts each screen (`ReelsPlaceholderScreen`, `ToTagScreen`, `SettingsPlaceholderScreen`) shows its title (finders on the English labels, no sleeps; taps settle the UI).
6. `patrol_test/database_test.dart › main`: a second on-device test, with no screens. It makes a `ProviderContainer`, creates a reel through `reelsRepositoryProvider` (the first query opens `taketray.sqlite` in the dev app's storage through drift_flutter, loading the bundled `libsqlite3.so`), closes `appDatabaseProvider`, makes a second container, and reads the reel back with `getById` and through `backupServiceProvider.exportJson`. Finding the reel in the second container proves the row reached the file.
7. `patrol_cli` prints the per-step results and the summary, and writes an HTML report to `app/build/app/reports/androidTests/connected/debug/index.html`. Because of `--no-uninstall` it neither uninstalls before installing nor after the run: `app.taketray.dev` ("TakeTray Dev" on the phone, next to the real TakeTray) and `app.taketray.dev.test` stay on the device (the dev app's data is still cleared by the orchestrator, step 3; the real app is untouched).

**Test videos (phase 3, D-77).** The two media tests put their own videos into the phone's media library, in `Movies/TakeTray Test/`:

8. `tool/make_test_video` › ffmpeg makes a 2 second, 160x90, 15 fps H.264 test pattern without audio (`testsrc2`, one encoder thread, `-fflags +bitexact`, no metadata), checks its size, and writes it as a base64 constant to `app/patrol_test/fixtures/test_video.dart` (`testVideoBytes`) through a temporary file and `tool/dart format`, so a missing ffmpeg or a failed run never changes the committed file.
9. **[bridge]** `MainActivity.configureFlutterEngine` › `DebugHooks.register(engine, applicationContext)`. In debug builds (`src/debug`) it makes a `TestMedia(contentResolver, packageName)` and sets a `TestMediaHandler` on the method channel `app.taketray/test_media`; release and profile builds register nothing. `insertVideo` runs on `Dispatchers.IO`: `ContentResolver.insert` of a row in `Movies/TakeTray Test/` with `IS_PENDING` 1, the bytes through `openOutputStream`, then `update` with `IS_PENDING` 0; a failure deletes the half-made row. `deleteVideo` queries the row (pending and trashed rows included), asks `TestMediaGuard.allowsDelete(relativePath, owner, ownPackage)` and deletes only when the folder is exactly `Movies/TakeTray Test/` and the owner is this app; otherwise the Dart call fails with code `refused` and nothing is deleted. `deleteLeftovers` does the same for every row in the folder.
10. `patrol_test/support/test_media.dart › TestMedia.start`: calls `deleteLeftovers` (a row in the folder that this app does not own fails the test with a message) and registers `addTearDown(cleanUp)`, which tries to delete every video the test inserted (each delete in its own try/catch, so one failure skips none), also when the test failed, then calls `deleteLeftovers` as a fallback and rethrows the first error. `app/test/patrol_support/test_media_test.dart` tests that loop with a fake channel (`tool/check` runs it). `insert()` calls `insertVideo` with `testVideoBytes` and a name `taketray-test-N.mp4`.
11. `patrol_test/support/system_dialogs.dart › allowAllInDialog`: Patrol's `grantPermissionWhenInUse` (it knows `permission_allow_all_button`); if One UI's dialog does not match, it waits for and taps `com.android.permissioncontroller:id/permission_allow_all_button` through `$.platform.android.getNativeViews` and `tap`, and a failure lists the resource ids on the screen (no text). `support/waiting.dart › waitFor` pumps frames until a condition is true (the record dot never lets `pumpAndSettle` finish).
12. `patrol_test/media_access_test.dart` (each test starts with cleared data, so without the permission):
    1. A fresh app shows "Manual mode" on To tag; **Allow full access** shows the system dialog; **Allow all** makes the banner go, on To tag and on Reels.
    2. `requestAccess` called on the bridge returns `full` within 20 seconds after the dialog is answered (so `MainActivity.onRequestPermissionsResult` passes the answer on; otherwise the call would hang).
    3. Limited access (written, **skipped** and to stay skipped until it verifies that the tile it picks in the system picker is the inserted test video; `--dart-define=LIMITED_ACCESS=true` only turns it on for developing that check): one test video is inserted, **Allow full access**, then `permission_allow_selected_button`, then the system picker's newest tile and `button_add`; the access level becomes `limited`, the banner still says "Manual mode", **Allow full access** shows the dialog again (`permission_allow_all_button`), **Allow all** removes the banner. The picker's ids are the AOSP ones; One UI may differ, in which case the failure lists the ids it saw.
13. `patrol_test/media_scan_test.dart`:
    1. The whole flow: access as in 12.1; `SettingsRepository.save` with `watchedFolders: ['Movies/TakeTray Test']`; Shooting mode on (the Reels switch; the test waits for the stored state); insert a video; open To tag: exactly one `TtClipTile`, whose `ClipThumbnailImage` is the tile's image and resolves to a picture without an error; delete the video, `pressHome` and `openApp`: To tag becomes empty ("All caught up"); Shooting mode off, insert a second video, To tag's three-dots menu › **Add earlier videos** › the **Today** row says "1 new" › **Choose dates…** › the date picker opens on this month › tap today's day twice (today to today, one day) › **Find videos** › the dialog says "1 video found" and no tile is there yet › **Add**: one tile › **Undo**: no tile (D-78; every query still names only `Movies/TakeTray Test`).
    2. The folder query: `videosAddedAfter` and `videosShotSince` find the video for `Movies/TakeTray Test`, `movies/takeTray test`, `MOVIES/TAKETRAY TEST` and `/Movies/TakeTray Test/` (so `COLLATE NOCASE` works on the real MediaProvider), and nothing for `Movies/TakeTray` or `Movies/TakeTray Test/deeper` (an exact folder, never a prefix or a deeper folder). The test never asks about `Movies` itself or any broad folder, because with full access that would return the owner's own videos; every query a Patrol test makes names only `Movies/TakeTray Test` (in other case) or those two folders that cannot hold owner media.
    3. Generation: the volume's generation read before the insert is below the new row's `generationAdded` (the scan finds `GENERATION_ADDED > baseline`), and the volume's generation read after is at least the row's.
14. Compile check without a phone: `tool/patrol build android` builds the debug app and the test APK (Dart and Kotlin); `tool/flutter analyze patrol_test` checks the Dart.

### Accessibility check in component tests (`expectTtAccessible`)

Called by component tests after pumping a widget (`packages/taketray_ui/test/support/tt_accessibility.dart`). See D-62.

1. `expectTtAccessible`: turns semantics on, then expects, in order, `androidTapTargetGuideline`, `labeledTapTargetGuideline`, `ttTextContrastGuideline` and, only when `Platform.isMacOS`, Flutter's `textContrastGuideline`. Semantics are disposed even when one fails.
2. `tt_text_contrast.dart › TtTextContrastGuideline.evaluate` → `measureTtTextContrast`, per render view:
   1. `_collectTargets`: walks the semantics tree exactly as Flutter's `MinimumTextContrastGuideline` does (skips invisible, merged, hidden and disabled nodes, routes and empty labels; for each label, the hit-testable `Text`/`EditableText` elements whose box meets the node), then `_textTarget` skips text more than 50 px off screen and records the font size and boldness Flutter would use, and the element's `RenderParagraph` or `RenderEditable`.
   2. `_collectShownTexts`: adds every other hit-testable, non-empty `Text` or `EditableText` in the view (text merged into a parent label, or excluded from semantics), unless `_effectiveSemantics` (the node it ends up in after merging) is disabled, hidden or invisible, or it is off screen. A text render object is never added twice.
   3. Collects every `RenderParagraph` and `RenderEditable` in the view and keeps their spans.
   4. `_restyleAll(_Paint.hidden)` gives each one a copy of its spans with the text colour transparent, shadows removed and any `decorationColor` transparent. A span with a `foreground`/`background` paint or a `backgroundColor`, or a run with no colour (own or inherited), throws `StateError`. Layout, compositing bits and paint are flushed; `_shoot` takes the view at one pixel per dp (as Flutter's guideline): the background image.
   5. `_restyleAll(_Paint.boxes)`: the same with `backgroundColor` set to each span's own colour (children inherit it). Second image: each run is a solid box in the text colour, composited like a glyph would be.
   6. `_restyleAll(_Paint.probe)`: the boxes in the inverted colour. Third image.
   7. In `finally`: the original spans are put back and the frame is flushed again, so nothing later sees the restyled text, also when a step threw.
   8. `_worstPair`, per target, inside its paint bounds:
      1. Box pixels are those the second or third image changed. A pixel with a 4-neighbour that is not a box pixel is dropped; if that leaves none (tiny text), all box pixels are used.
      2. Each kept pixel gives a (second-image colour, background colour) pair. Pairs are ranked by contrast ratio (lowest first; equal ratios by colour value).
      3. The worst pairs are skipped while together they make up no more than 2 % (`ignoredShare`) of the kept pixels; the next pair is the sample.
      4. No box pixel at all (the text is clipped away, or fully transparent) gives the background against itself, 1:1.
3. Back in `evaluate`: each sample whose ratio is more than 0.01 under `MinimumTextContrastGuideline.targetContrastRatio(fontSize, bold:)` adds a failure naming the text, the ratio, the font size and both colours (`#rrggbb on #rrggbb`).

### Catalogue coverage (`widgetbook/test/catalog_coverage_test.dart`)

Runs inside `tool/check` (step "test widgetbook") and with `(cd widgetbook && ../tool/flutter test)`. It keeps the Widgetbook catalogue complete (D-42).

1. `widgetbook/test/catalog_coverage_test.dart › main`: lists the `*.dart` files directly in `packages/taketray_ui/lib/src/components/` (none if the folder does not exist yet).
2. Same test: for each name, checks that `widgetbook/lib/components/<name>.dart` exists.
3. `widgetbook/test/catalog_coverage_test.dart › _imports`: checks that `widgetbook/lib/catalog.dart` has an import of `components/<name>.dart` (`package:taketray_widgetbook/components/...`).
4. Same test: `expect(problems, isEmpty)` fails listing every missing file and every missing import; a component without a catalogue file therefore fails `tool/check`.
5. `widgetbook/lib/catalog.dart › ttCatalog`: the registered components, read by `widgetbook/lib/main.dart › TakeTrayCatalogue` (`Widgetbook.material`) when the catalogue runs.
